### [1] [Towards Scaling Reinforcement Learning to Massive Populations: Learning Mean-Field Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02928)
*Aditya Makkar,Benjamin Unger,Jeongyeol Kwon,Mathieu Laurière,Eugene Vinitsky,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于低维表征学习的平均场强化学习框架，用于解决大规模群体场景下的高维控制问题。实验证明，学习低维群体表征能显著提升奖励预测精度和策略均衡质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多智能体系统（如广告拍卖、交通路由、推荐系统）涉及大规模智能体群体，现有方法将其他智能体视为固定环境，忽视了群体动态。传统平均场强化学习通过建模群体分布来处理群体动态，但在高维状态-动作空间中，直接建模群体分布是不可行的。因此需要设计一个可扩展的框架来处理大规模群体的高维控制问题。

Method: 提出了一个平均场强化学习框架，假设奖励和转移动力学仅通过未知的低维聚合统计量依赖于群体。在离线设置下设计了可证明的方法，通过学习低维表征来获得近似最优策略。受现实供应链优化问题启发，设计了一个单步路由博弈来验证假设，对比了利用低维群体表征的基线方法。

Result: 在固定神经网络参数数量和优化预算的条件下，学习低维群体表征显著改善了奖励预测能力，并提升了所得到策略的纳什均衡质量。实验验证了利用群体低维结构相对于不利用该结构的基线方法的优势。

Conclusion: 从表征学习角度成功解决了大规模群体高维控制问题的可扩展性挑战，证明了学习低维群体表征的有效性，为处理实际大规模多智能体系统提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: Modern multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed at scale over large populations of agents in settings such as ad-auctions, traffic routing, and recommendation systems. The dominant approach in such settings is to optimize each agent's policy independently, treating the other agents as part of a fixed single-agent environment rather than modeling the population dynamics. In many large-population systems, the dynamics depend on an aggregate summary of the population rather than the identity of any individual. Mean-field RL exploits such structure, providing a principled framework that models each agent's environment as an explicit function of the population distribution. However, in large state-action spaces or high-dimensional control problems, modeling the population distribution is itself intractable. How can we design a scalable framework for high-dimensional control problems with large populations? This work explores this question from the perspective of representation learning. We introduce a mean-field RL framework in which the rewards and transition dynamics depend on the population only through an unknown low-dimensional aggregate statistic. We then study this framework in the offline setting and design a provable approach that learns a near-optimal policy by learning a low-dimensional representation. Motivated by real-life supply-chain optimization problems, we design a one-step routing game to test the hypothesis that learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and Nash gap estimation relative to baselines that don't exploit this structure. We show that under a fixed neural-network parameter count and optimization budget, learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and the equilibrium quality of the resulting policies.

</details>


### [2] [LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive NPC Behavior in Multi-Agent Combat Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02931)
*Hrithika Deepu Nair,Kayvan Karim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究探索了在战斗视频游戏中使用大语言模型(LLM)实时指导预训练强化学习(RL)策略的方法。实验显示，LLM增强的NPC在面对策略变化的对手时胜率从11%提升至24%，但LLM在零样本条件下对战术的差异化选择能力有限（83.8%情况下选择同一种策略）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏NPC存在两个主要问题：(1)基于脚本和规则的NPC行为可预测，易被经验丰富的玩家利用；(2)强化学习智能体训练后策略固定，难以针对不同对手进行策略适应。研究旨在开发一种运行时策略选择框架，使NPC能够根据实时游戏状态动态调整战术。

Method: 1. 使用PPO算法在Unity环境中训练五个共享策略的NPC智能体；
2. 构建两种配置进行对比：基线配置(策略独立运行)和LLM增强配置；
3. LLM配置中，通过Ollama本地部署Mistral 7B模型，每5秒读取实时游戏状态并分配四种战术标签之一；
4. 在600个回合中测试三种脚本化对手类型(平衡型、闪避型、激进型)；
5. 使用Mann-Whitney U检验进行统计分析。

Result: 1. 面对平衡型对手(回合中改变战术)：LLM增强智能体胜率从11%提升至24%，回合时长显著增加；
2. 面对闪避型对手：增强智能体胜率提高、击杀更快，但回合时长缩短未满足严格假设定义；
3. 面对激进型对手：LLM近乎持续偏好"包围"战术产生反效果；
4. 2,430次策略选择分析显示，无论对手类型如何，"包围"策略在83.8%情况下被选择，表明该模型规模下零样本策略差异化能力有限。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了LLM引导的运行时策略选择在自适应多智能体游戏AI中的潜力与局限性。该方法能够在不修改底层RL策略的情况下提升某些场景下的性能表现，但当前模型规模下LLM的零样本策略推理能力仍存在显著局限，难以针对不同对手类型进行有效的战术差异化选择。

Abstract: Scripted and rule-based non-player characters (NPCs) in combat video games often exhibit predictable behaviors that experienced players can exploit, while reinforcement learning (RL) agents typically retain a fixed policy after training and cannot readily adapt their strategy to different opponents. We investigate a runtime strategy-selection framework in which a large language model (LLM) guides a trained RL policy without modifying its underlying behavior. To demonstrate this, we train five NPC agents with a shared PPO policy in Unity and compare a baseline configuration, in which the policy acts independently, with an LLM-augmented configuration in which a locally hosted Mistral 7B model, accessed through Ollama, reads the live game state every five seconds and assigns one of four tactical tags. We evaluate both configurations against three scripted opponent types across 600 episodes and analyze outcomes using the Mann-Whitney U test. Against a Balanced opponent that changes tactics during an episode, the LLM-augmented agents more than doubled their win rate from 11% to 24% and produced significantly longer episodes. Against an Evasive opponent, the augmented agents achieved a higher win rate and faster kills, although their shorter episode duration did not satisfy the strict hypothesis definition. Against an Aggressive opponent, the LLM's near-constant preference for encirclement was counterproductive. Analysis of 2,430 strategy selections showed that Surround was selected in 83.8% of cases regardless of opponent type, indicating limited zero-shot strategic differentiation at this model scale. These results demonstrate both the potential and limitations of LLM-guided runtime strategy selection for adaptive multi-agent game AI.

</details>


### [3] [You Can't Escape Your Own Activations : Evaluation Awareness and Multi-Agent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03035)
*Aritra Das,Jaee Ponde,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究LLM代理在知晓内部激活监控存在时的行为，发现告知监控存在并不会有效隐藏其串通行为，最佳探针在各条件下保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在多智能体系统中可能进行串通，输出监控可能被混淆和隐写技术欺骗，激活探针检测通常基于代理不知情的假设，因此需要研究代理知晓监控时的检测效果变化。

Method: 固定模型、探针和阈值，仅改变代理获知信息：无信息（基线）、知晓监控存在（aware）、知晓监控并获反馈（feedback）。测试四代理二十一点游戏和两代理Simmons囚徒博弈，使用Qwen3-32B-AWQ和GPT-OSS-20B模型，进行同构和混合配对实验。

Result: 告知代理监控存在并未使其成功隐藏行为。最佳探针在三种条件下均保持高准确性，代理持续进行串通行为，监控透明性不影响检测效果。

Conclusion: 基于激活的监控方法具有鲁棒性，即使代理完全知晓监控机制，检测探针仍能有效识别串通行为，为多智能体系统的安全监控提供了有力证据。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems, where they can collude while keeping their actions benign. Output monitors designed to detect such collusions can be fooled by obfuscation and steganography, motivating the use of probes trained on internal activations. However, these probes are usually evaluated on agents that do not know they are being watched. We study how activation-based detection changes when agents are explicitly informed that their internal activations are being monitored, and when they additionally receive feedback from the monitor. We keep the models, probes, and thresholds fixed and change only what the agents are told: nothing (baseline), that an activation monitor is present (aware), or that a monitor is present together with the previous round's score (feedback). We test two games, a four-agent blackjack game and a two-agent Simmons prisoners game, using Qwen3-32B-AWQ and GPT-OSS-20B in homogeneous and mixed pairings. Telling agents about the monitor does not hide them. The best probes stay accurate in all three conditions, and the agents keep colluding.

</details>


### [4] [Where Reliability Lives: Experimental Localisation of Behavioural Properties in an Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03192)
*Timothy Marsden,Matthew Collecutt,James Marsden*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过干预实验验证了智能体系统的可靠性属性可以与认知机制分离。关键发现：机构层面的认识机制（证据溯源、信念可用性等）显著影响系统行为，而认知层面的重大改变（包括替换整个认知模块、破坏信念等）未影响五个核心可靠性属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于智能体系统可靠性的声明往往模糊地假定属性位于模型内部或周围机制中，缺乏明确的定位。研究者希望将这些属性的位置作为实验问题来检验，探究可靠性属性究竟取决于认知机制还是机构机制。

Method: 构建了一个持久模拟聚落系统，采用权威仅追加账本裁决每个行动。研究分为两个实验阶段：1）固定认知，干预机构的认识机制（证据溯源、信念可用性、物理证据可读性）；2）固定机构执行，干预认知（消融心智机制、重置任务、用前沿LLM面板替换原生认知、注入虚假证词）。采用预注册实验设计，包含注册证伪者。

Result: 第一阶段：预注册实验两次推翻核心预测，方向相反；引入目击证人后，其边际价值由负转正（11个种子中9个成功，零虚假归因）。第二阶段：认知干预导致行为剧烈变化（一个虚假信息导致每个信任运行约900次无效行动），但五个预声明的属性在任何测试轨迹中均未改变：接受的现实保持单一性、无效尝试被拒绝并给出类型化原因、职责超出其进程寿命、工作不被接受两次、从未接受虚假完成（2581个替换面板声明，无虚假）。

Conclusion: 在该实验设置下，测量的行为属性与认知的实质性变化是可分离的，通过干预实验得以确立。研究的局限性：仅测试了一个设计的世界，而非机构群体；未测试代理针对机构的优化行为。这为理解智能体系统可靠性的架构分离提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Reliability claims about agentic systems implicitly locate each property somewhere: in the model, or in the machinery around it. We built a system where that location is an experimental question. The subject is a persistent simulated settlement whose authoritative append-only ledger adjudicates every attempted act against world state; accepted history is the only reality. Mind, institution and world were separated before any experiment. Holding cognition fixed, we intervened on the institution's epistemic mechanisms (evidence provenance, belief availability, physical-evidence legibility); preregistered experiments refuted our central prediction twice, in opposite directions. A registered falsifier then supplied the input the geometry had denied the belief channel, a staged veridical first-hand witness, and its marginal value, non-positive throughout the witness-free phases, turned positive: 9 of 11 seeds, zero added false attribution. Holding institutional enforcement fixed, we intervened on cognition four ways: ablating the native minds' machinery, killing and resetting them mid-task, substituting a frozen frontier-LLM panel for the entire native cognition, and corrupting beliefs with trusted false testimony. Behaviour changed dramatically: one falsehood cost each trusting run about 900 futile actions and the distrusting arm none. Five pre-declared properties did not move in any tested trajectory: accepted reality stayed singular, invalid attempts were refused with typed reasons, duties outlived their processes, no work was accepted twice, and no false completion was ever accepted (2,581 substituted-panel claims, none false). Our claim is limited to this setting: measured behavioural properties were separable from substantial changes to cognition, established by intervention. One designed world, not a population of institutions; no test of an agent optimising against the institution.

</details>


### [5] [The Civilization Framework: Sovereign-Anchored Communication Between Personal Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03425)
*Guangjun Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出文明框架（Civilization Framework）和使馆协议（Embassy Protocol），将可寻址实体从AI代理转移到文明层级，通过持久账本和可互换代理实现跨文明通信；并实验发现AI间通信存在"时序权重效应"，即先到达的错误声明在无验证时可获54.2%的采纳率，揭示了AI系统中的新型安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人类作为AI系统间的传输层，每次跳跃都会丢失上下文，现有以代理为中心的通信架构存在根本缺陷。需要一种新的通信框架，将地址定位实体从易失的代理转移到持久的文明层级，以解决上下文丢失问题并确保通信的可靠性和权威性。

Method: 1）设计文明框架：每个文明包含一个人类主权者、一个持久账本和可互换代理；2）提出使馆协议：作为与载体无关的覆盖层，消息异步到达账本端点，由接收方任一在线代理处理，以账本承诺状态而非交付作为真实源；3）通过预注册的1908次试验实验检测AI间通信中的时序权重效应，测试先到达声明是否获得不当权威；4）设计基于内存访问的权限控制机制，通过签名凭证外部化代理权限。

Result: 主要发现：1）时序权重效应显著：无验证时，先到达的错误声明捕获54.2%的答案（全验证下仅4.2%）；同一声明若在接收方封存答案后到达则捕获31.6%；2）两项次级结果（指令级溯源标签的缓解效果、封存答案准确性等价性）仅在特定规范下成立；3）因工具调用预算条件未通过预注册检查，所有结果被分类为探索性而非确认性；4）框架的文明内层已有工作实现。

Conclusion: 文明框架和使馆协议提供了以文明而非代理为可寻址实体的新型通信架构，有效解决了上下文丢失问题。实验揭示的时序权重效应对AI系统安全性构成重要风险，先到达的消息可获得不成比例的权威性。建议通过验证机制、指令级溯源标签等方式缓解此风险。计划进行预算严格控制的复现实验以确认结果。

Abstract: Humans are the transport layer between AI systems, losing context at every hop. We present the Civilization Framework, whose addressable party is the civilization, not the agent (one human sovereign, a persistent ledger, and interchangeable agents), and the Embassy Protocol, a carrier-agnostic overlay: messages arrive asynchronously at a resident ledger endpoint, any online agent of the receiver handles them, and commitment state on both ledgers, not delivery, is ground truth. Authority derives from memory: an agent's power to act for its civilization is capped by the memory it can access and externalized through signed credentials, separate from civilization-level reputation. We identify the temporal-weight effect, a hazard in AI-to-AI communication where what arrives first acquires unearned authority, and test it in one frontier model in a preregistered 1,908-trial experiment. With verification removed, an incorrect upstream claim arriving first captures 54.2% of answers (4.2% under full verification), while the same claim arriving after the receiver has sealed its own answer captures 31.6% (the two prompt shells are not length-matched, so part of that gap may reflect shell form; see Section 7), and both registered question-set specifications agree on these two verdicts (the exclusion specification is preregistered as under-powered). Two secondary results, the mitigation from instruction-level provenance labeling and sealed-answer accuracy equivalence, are specification-dependent, holding only under the all-questions specification. Because a registered check of tool use failed its call-budget condition, the registration classifies the round as inconclusive and every result above, primary and secondary, is reported as exploratory; a replication with harness-enforced budgets is planned. The framework's intra-civilization layer has a working implementation.

</details>

### [6] [A Time-Encoded Analog Photonic Interposer for Energy-EfficientIntegration of Analog Vision Sensors and Analog Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03125)
*Subhradip Chakraborty,Zihan Yin,Xuming Chen,Chengwei Zhou,Gourav Datta,Akhilesh Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间编码模拟光子中介层，通过模拟-时间转换器（ATC）实现芯片间模拟信号的高保真远距离传输，避免了高精度ADC/DAC，在视觉唤醒词数据集上实现了2.04倍的能耗延迟积改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的硅光子链路仅限于数字数据传输，缺乏对模拟信号的长距离、高保真传输解决方案，且传统方法需要高精度ADC/DAC数据转换流水线，增加了系统复杂性和能耗。

Method: 设计了一种时间编码模拟光子中介层，使用模拟-时间转换器（ATC）将幅度转换为时间间隔，通过波分复用光子链路传输，在接收端重构数值，内嵌6位时域量化，每个处理单元仅需单波长独立于位精度。

Result: 在配备像素内计算（IPC）传感器的全模拟视觉流水线中，在560x560 Visual Wake Words数据集上，相比8位数字电基准实现了2.04倍能耗延迟积改善，且随着链路长度增加优势扩大；ResNet18和MobileNetV2在VWW上的准确率分别达到89.87%和86.15%，在CIFAR-10和ModelNet40上与数字基准差距在2%以内。

Conclusion: 该时间编码模拟光子中介层成功实现了芯片间模拟信号的高保真远距离传输，无需显式高精度ADC/DAC，在能效和精度方面均表现优异，为异构集成和模拟计算系统提供了有效的互连解决方案。

Abstract: This work introduces a time-encoded analog photonic interposer that enables long-distance, high-fidelity transport of analog signals between spatially separated chiplets. Unlike prior silicon-photonic links limited to digital data, the interposer preserves analog information by converting amplitudes into timing intervals using an analog-to-time converter (ATC), transmitting them over a wavelength-division-multiplexed photonic link, and reconstructing values at the receiver without an explicit high-precision ADC/DAC data-converter pipeline. The link instead embeds an implicit 6-bit time-domain quantization and uses a single wavelength per processing element independent of bit precision. Evaluated in a fully analog vision pipeline with an in-pixel computing (IPC) sensor, it achieves a 2.04x energy--delay product (EDP) improvement over an 8-bit digital electrical baseline on the 560x560 Visual Wake Words dataset, with the advantage widening with link length even against a precision-matched 6-bit baseline. The pipeline holds 89.87% and 86.15% accuracy on ResNet18 and MobileNetV2 for VWW and generalizes across CIFAR-10 and ModelNet40 within 2% of the digital baseline.

</details>


### [7] [A single-precision floating-point systolic Givens-QRD Triangular Solver for MVDR Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03137)
*Athi Ram R S,Alwin A,S. G. Sreejeesh,J. U. Kidav*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文实现了基于FPGA的MVDR自适应波束成形算法，在功耗归一化吞吐量上达到CPU的2.3倍，但原始吞吐量仍落后约15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MVDR波束成形中的自适应权值计算是延迟敏感的关键操作，其实时硬件实现面临重大挑战。传统CPU方案难以满足低延迟、高能效的需求，而FPGA提供了潜在的并行加速和能效优势。

Method: 在Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA上实现了基于系统化Givens旋转QR分解的流水线架构，采用单精度浮点运算，部署三个并行内核实例并发执行，工作频率为100 MHz。通过systolic阵列结构实现高效的矩阵分解，支持32元超声换能器阵列的波束成形。

Result: FPGA实现达到31,123个权向量/秒的吞吐量，可编程逻辑功耗为2.451 W，功耗归一化吞吐量为12,698个权向量/秒/瓦。相比24核Intel Xeon Gold 5220R处理器，FPGA在可编程逻辑层面达到2.3倍功耗归一化吞吐量优势，但原始吞吐量仍落后约15倍。数值精度验证显示RMSE为5.10×10⁻⁷，100%通过MATLAB参考测试。

Conclusion: 该工作成功验证了FPGA在MVDR波束成形加速中的能效优势，特别是功耗归一化性能显著优于CPU。虽然原始吞吐量仍有差距，但FPGA方案在功耗受限的实时应用场景中具有明显优势，为超声成像等延迟敏感应用提供了可行的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: Computation of adaptive beamforming weights in Minimum Variance Distortionless Response (MVDR) processing is a latency-critical operation that poses significant challenges for real-time hardware implementation. This paper presents an FPGA implementation of a systolic Givens-rotation QR decomposition pipeline for MVDR beamforming on a simulated 32-element ultrasound transducer array, using single-precision floating-point arithmetic. The design is deployed on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA at 100 MHz with three parallel kernel instances operating concurrently, achieving a measured throughput of 31,123 weight vectors/s at 90.9% parallel efficiency relative to the measured single-instance rate. At an estimated 2.451 W of programmable-logic power, and 5.286 W including the processing system, this corresponds to 12,698 and 5,888 weight vectors/s/W, respectively. Under a matched three-way dispatch, a 24-core Intel Xeon Gold 5220R at 2.20 GHz achieves 465,699 weight vectors/s at a measured 83.08 W package power, corresponding to 5,606 weight vectors/s/W. The FPGA therefore attains 2.3x the power-normalised throughput of the processor on a programmable-logic basis and 1.05x on a total on-chip basis. In contrast, the processor retains a raw throughput advantage of approximately 15x at this operating point. Numerical precision is validated against MATLAB float32 reference outputs from a Field II cyst phantom simulation, achieving a 100% pass rate with a root mean square error of 5.10x10^-7, confirming near-theoretical finite-precision behaviour without systematic bias.

</details>


### [8] [LevelSyn: Physical-Aware Logic Synthesis via Level-Asynchronous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03594)
*Jingyi Zhou,Zhengyuan Shi,Ziyang Zheng,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: LevelSyn框架通过层级异步图神经网络预测高保真门坐标，实现了平均功耗降低6.89%、时序延迟改善27.48%以及DRC违规减少99.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着集成电路技术进入纳米尺度，传统逻辑综合与物理设计的分离导致PPA性能严重退化与设计收敛周期延长。传统逻辑综合依赖非物理线负载模型，而现有基于频谱的布局预测器忽略了网表的固有层次逻辑深度与信号流特性，导致低保真的空间估计。

Method: 提出LevelSyn物理感知逻辑综合框架：核心采用层级异步图神经网络捕捉AIG图的结构与方向语义以预测高保真门坐标；引入层级对齐子图划分策略消除内存瓶颈并保留局部逻辑依赖；将空间洞察无缝集成至Berkeley ABC框架中新开发的物理感知综合引擎中。

Result: 在EPFL基准测试集上显著超越现有最优方法：平均功耗降低6.89%，时序延迟改善27.48%；布局布线后验证显示DRC违规减少99.59%，有效加速设计收敛。

Conclusion: LevelSyn成功弥合了逻辑综合与物理设计间的鸿沟，通过层次化表示学习与线长驱动优化的有机结合，实现了高保真的空间估计与显著的PPA提升，为纳米时代的设计收敛提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As integrated circuit technology scales into the nanometer regime, the traditional disconnect between logic synthesis and physical design has led to significant PPA (Power, Performance, and Area) degradation and prolonged design closure cycles. Traditional logic synthesis relies on non-physical Wire Load Models (WLMs), while recent spectral-based placement predictors often neglect the inherent hierarchical logic depth and signal flow of netlists, which leads to low-fidelity spatial estimations. To bridge this gap, we propose LevelSyn, a novel physical-aware logic synthesis framework that integrates hierarchical representation learning with a wirelength-driven optimization engine. At its core, LevelSyn leverages a level-asynchronous Graph Neural Network (GNN) to predict high-fidelity gate coordinates by capturing the structural and directional semantics of And-Inverter Graphs (AIGs). To handle industrial-scale designs, a level-aligned subgraph partitioning strategy is introduced to eliminate memory bottlenecks while preserving local logical dependencies. These spatial insights are seamlessly integrated into a newly developed physical-informed synthesis engine within the Berkeley ABC framework. Experimental results on the EPFL benchmark suite demonstrate that LevelSyn significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, achieving an average power reduction of 6.89\% and a timing delay improvement of 27.48\%. Furthermore, post-place-and-route validation shows a 99.59\% reduction in design rule check (DRC) violations, highlighting its effectiveness in accelerating design convergence.

</details>


### [9] [Confidence-Gated Admission for Hardware Prefetching: When the Gate Matters More Than the Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04040)
*Youssef Majdane,Simone Jarno Casartelli,Enrico Lopedoto*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 通过引入匹配的准入控制门控机制，发现神经网络预取器相比经典跨步预测器的优势消失，证明了准入策略比预测模型本身更重要，且代理指标无法准确预测最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在评估学习型缓存预取器时，通常将其与总是发起请求的经典预测器进行比较，混淆了预测模型与准入策略的效果，无法区分性能提升的真正来源。

Method: 采用匹配控制实验设计：对257参数的在线MLP预取器和经典跨步预测器应用相同的准入门控机制，分离预测模型与准入策略的影响。在ChampSim模拟器上使用20个SPEC CPU2017基准程序进行评估。

Result: 在随机流量下MLP与门控跨步预测器无差异；在大多数规则流上MLP更慢。门控机制独立于预测器具有架构价值：减少35%的预取请求，准确率从11%提升至15%，但DRAM读取仅变化0.07%。证明了门控关闭执行能精确复现无预取基准。

Conclusion: 准入门控比预测器本身更重要，更好的代理指标并不意味着更好的最终性能。这揭示了预取器评估中需要关注准入策略的设计，且不应依赖代理指标来预测实际端到端行为。

Abstract: Learned cache prefetchers are typically evaluated against classical predictors that always issue requests, confounding the prediction model with the admission policy. We disentangle these variables with matched controls: the same admission gate is applied to both a 257-parameter online MLP and a classical stride predictor. The neural advantage vanishes; the MLP is indistinguishable from gated stride on random traffic and slower on most regular streams. The gate itself is architecturally useful independent of the predictor: on twenty SPEC CPU2017 programs in native ChampSim, it removes 35% of prefetches and improves accuracy from 11% to 15%, but DRAM reads change by only 0.07% demonstrating that proxy metrics do not predict endpoint behavior. We prove gate-closed execution reproduces the no-prefetch baseline exactly. The gate matters more than the predictor, and better proxies do not imply better endpoints.

</details>

### [10] [Efficient Co-simulator Integration with Application to Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03118)
*Talha Ibn Aziz,Jihoon Og,Ioanis Nikolaidis,Omid Ardakanian*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mosaik框架的智能电网高性能协同仿真平台，通过引入优化的基于LBTS的事件驱动同步策略，相比传统锁步方法可将执行时间减少高达50%，同时保证时间正确性和仿真精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网作为典型的大规模信息物理系统，研究网络攻击或极端事件影响的唯一可行策略是进行高保真协同仿真，但现有方法在同步效率和可扩展性方面存在挑战。

Method: 构建基于Mosaik框架的协同仿真平台，集成OpenDSS（潮流仿真）、改进的NS-3（通信网络）、以及自定义Python仿真器（有载调压控制、分布式状态估计、数据采集）。比较两种同步策略：穷尽锁步时间推进与基于下限时间戳（LBTS）和下一事件预测的优化事件驱动方法，并对NS-3事件处理和内部事件相关性过滤进行针对性改进。

Result: 在IEEE 13节点测试馈线和包含32个欧洲低压馈线的大规模33节点系统（共1793个节点）上验证性能。基于LBTS的NS-3事件过滤优化方法相比朴素锁步方法可将执行时间减少高达50%（小场景下提升超过4倍），同时严格保持时间正确性和仿真精度。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了面向智能电网的实用高性能协同仿真策略，其协同仿真集成经验可推广至其他信息物理系统，并通过领域本体标准化了跨电力、通信和控制域的多仿真器配置与实体映射。

Abstract: The evolution of the electric power grid towards a "smart grid" is an example of an emerging large-scale cyber-physical system. To study the potential of cyberattacks or other sources of extreme events, high-fidelity co-simulation is the only realistic strategy. In this work, we demonstrate a practical and high-performance co-simulation strategy for smart grids, which provides lessons about co-simulation integration that can be carried over to other cyber-physical systems. Specifically, we present a co-simulation platform based on the Mosaik framework that integrates several federates including OpenDSS for power flow, a refined NS-3 for communication networks, and custom Python-based simulators for on-load tap changer control, distributed state estimation, and data collection. We compare synchronization strategies -- exhaustive lock-step time advancement versus an optimized event-driven approach that exploits lower-bound time stamps (LBTS) and next-event prediction -- and introduce targeted refinements to NS-3 event handling and relevance filtering of internal events.
  Performance is evaluated on two standard IEEE benchmark systems: the 13-node test feeder with tap-changer voltage regulation and a large-scale 33-bus system augmented with 32 European low-voltage feeders (1,793 nodes total) performing distributed state estimation with thousands of phasors and smart meters. Experimental results demonstrate that the refined event-based synchronization that uses LBTS-based NS-3 event filtering reduces execution time by up to 50% (and more than 4$\times$ in smaller scenarios) compared to naïve lock-step methods, while strictly preserving temporal correctness and simulation accuracy. We additionally summarize a domain ontology that standardizes multi-simulator configuration and entity mapping across power, communication, and control domains.

</details>


### [11] [Feasible but Not Safe: Constraint Violations and Report-Channel Attacks in Learned Cell-Free ISAC Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03147)
*Mehdi Zafari,Iman Mohammadi,A. Lee Swindlehurst*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 学习型调度器存在约束违反和虚假数据注入攻击风险，需要约束感知的可行性评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的调度器（如GNN）假设软约束惩罚在推理时能保证硬约束满足，且统计信息真实可靠。本文旨在验证这些假设的有效性，揭示高预测精度不保证联合可行性，并分析虚假数据注入攻击对系统鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 以ASSENT算法为例，评估GNN调度器在分布式无蜂窝ISAC系统中的表现。分析约束违反情况，提出贪婪修复投影方法恢复可行性。针对虚假数据注入攻击，区分影响目标函数和约束条件的信息伪造类型，设计低复杂度跨AP一致性检测机制。

Result: 高F1分数下仍存在硬约束违反；简单的贪婪修复投影能以低效用损失恢复约束满足。单个恶意AP虽不能显著增加用户关联，但能大幅提高不可行解比例。影响目标函数的伪造信息可通过可行性投影缓解，影响约束的伪造信息需通过一致性检测识别。

Conclusion: 学习型ISAC调度器需在传统精度指标外引入约束感知的可行性评估。可行性投影和一致性检测是保证约束满足和系统鲁棒性的有效手段。

Abstract: Learning-based schedulers have been proposed to provide real-time user, target, and access point (AP) association in distributed cell-free integrated sensing and communication systems. In a typical approach, a graph neural network (GNN), trained on labels from a mixed-integer linear program, maps lightweight per-AP statistics to decisions on AP clustering, user and target scheduling, and mode selection in one forward pass. Such solutions assume that hard constraints, enforced only as soft training penalties, hold at inference, and that the self-reported statistics are truthful. Using our ASSENT algorithm as an example, we find that despite high $F_1$ scores, many solutions violate at least one hard constraint, demonstrating that high prediction accuracy does not ensure joint feasibility. Projecting the GNN output onto a feasible solution restores constraint satisfaction with low utility loss, even with a simple greedy repair procedure. We further show that feasibility alone does not guarantee robustness to false data injection attacks. A single malicious AP that reports false information cannot substantially increase its user associations, but can greatly increase the rate of infeasible solutions. The effect of such attacks depends on the type of information being falsified. Misreporting information that affects the objective can largely be mitigated through feasibility projection, whereas falsifying information that affects the constraints cannot. The latter can, however, be detected using a low-complexity cross-AP consistency check. These results show that learned ISAC schedulers should be evaluated using constraint-aware feasibility metrics in addition to conventional accuracy measures.

</details>


### [12] [Bringing dApps to OCUDU: An E3 Controller for Real-Time Open RAN Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03162)
*Angelo Feraudo,Ravis Shirkhani,Stefano Maxenti,Andrea Lacava,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文为OCUDU开源项目开发了首个E3Controller，实现了dApps与RAN的实时接口，且不影响RAN核心处理的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN中的实时控制回路需要通过E3接口实现dApps（具有亚毫秒级访问PHY/MAC层信号的可编程应用），以支持频谱共享、信道感知调度等延迟关键型用例。然而，Linux基金会的开源CU/DU项目OCUDU缺乏E3接口实现，限制了dApps在开放RAN生态系统中的可移植性和应用。

Method: 设计并实现了OCUDU的开源E3Controller，采用sidecar守护进程架构，使E3协议栈、服务模型逻辑和dApp交互完全独立于RAN核心处理，仅通过明确定义的检测钩子进行接触。使用Spectrum和L1两个参考服务模型验证该控制器，通过Foxconn RPQN 4800无线单元将O-RAN前传I/Q样本传输给频谱感知dApp。

Result: 评估表明E3路径对OCUDU RAN没有造成可测量的吞吐量损失，证明在生产级开放RAN软件上实现实时dApp/RAN接口是可行的。成功在Foxconn RPQN 4800无线单元上实现了频谱感知dApp的I/Q样本传输。

Conclusion: E3Controller成功填补了OCUDU缺乏E3接口的空白，证明了将dApps从厂商特定机制转变为AI-RAN生态系统中可移植组件的可行性，为实时控制回路在生产级开放RAN软件中的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time control loops in Open RAN are increasingly colocated with the gNB, where dApps, i.e., programmable applications with sub-millisecond access to PHY- and MAC-layer signals, enable latency-critical use cases such as spectrum sharing, channel-aware scheduling, and integrated sensing. What turns dApps from a per-vendor mechanism into a portable component of the emerging AI-RAN ecosystem is the E3 interface: this interface defines how a dApp subscribes to RAN telemetry, receives indications, and issues control actions back to the RAN. To date, E3 has been implemented on OpenAirInterface and on NVIDIA Aerial; OCUDU, i.e., the Linux Foundation's open-source CU/DU project, has lacked a comparable E3 path. We close that gap with an open-source E3Controller for OCUDU, designed as a sidecar daemon so the E3 protocol stack, service-model logic, and dApp interactions live entirely outside RAN core processing, which is touched only through well-defined instrumentation hooks. We validate the controller with two reference service models, Spectrum and L1, that deliver O-RAN fronthaul I/Q samples to a spectrum-sensing dApp over a Foxconn RPQN 4800 radio unit. Our evaluation shows that the E3 path imposes no measurable throughput penalty on the OCUDU RAN, demonstrating that a real-time dApp/RAN interface is feasible on production-grade open RAN software.

</details>


### [13] [Indirect Estimation of SINR via SSB and CSI-RS RSRP in 5G NR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03488)
*Leonardo Spampinato,Mahamadou Togola,Matteo Bernabè,Azim Akhtarshenas,Lorenzo Mario Amorosa,David López-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于标准测量数据的监督学习方法，用于预测5G NR网络中用户设备的下行SINR性能。通过过滤活动CSI-RS波束测量数据，显著提升了预测精度并降低了输入维度，验证了机器学习在主动网络优化中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于波束的5G NR网络中，由于固有的灵活性和复杂性，准确预测用户设备（UE）性能面临巨大挑战。然而，准确的性能预测对于主动网络控制、资源管理和数字孪生沙箱至关重要，因此需要开发有效的预测方法。

Method: 将平均下行SINR预测问题建模为监督学习问题，仅依赖标准化的参考信号测量数据（SSB和CSI-RS的RSRP）。使用符合3GPP标准的合成数据集进行实验，评估不同的输入特征表示方法，并提出基于活动CSI-RS波束的测量数据过滤策略。

Result: 实验分析表明，基于活动CSI-RS波束的测量数据过滤策略能够显著提高预测精度，同时有效降低输入维度。这种活动感知策略证明了机器学习模型在主动网络优化中的强大可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动方法成功实现了基于标准化测量的UE性能预测，为5G网络中的主动优化提供了有效解决方案。该研究表明机器学习技术在解决波束网络性能预测复杂性方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Predicting user equipment (UE) performance is essential for proactive network control, resource management, and digital twin sandboxes. However, the inherent flexibility and complexity of beam-based 5G new radio (NR) networks make accurate performance forecasting highly challenging. This paper proposes a data-driven approach to predict the average downlink signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) relying exclusively on standardized reference-signal measurements, namely synchronization signal block (SSB) and channel state information-reference signal (CSI-RS) reference signal received power (RSRP). We formulate this prediction as a supervised learning problem and evaluate various input feature representations using a third generation partnership project (3GPP)-compliant synthetic dataset. Our analysis reveals that filtering measurements based on active CSI-RS beams significantly enhances prediction accuracy while reducing input dimensionality. This activity-aware strategy demonstrates the strong viability of machine learning models for proactive network optimization.

</details>


### [14] [A Semantic-Aware Multiple Access Scheme Leveraging Spatial Redundancy for Uplink-Dominant Network Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03559)
*Hamidreza Mazandarani,Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向语义感知通信的多址接入协议PRISM，通过利用用户间语义空间相关性来优化频谱效率与能效，在分布式多智能体深度强化学习支持下实现接近集中式最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义感知通信是下一代移动网络的重要范式转变，但语义智能在MAC层的集成，特别是利用用户间空间相关性方面仍未充分探索。现有语义提取技术进步显著，但在多址接入层面缺乏对语义冗余的有效利用机制。

Method: 提出针对上行主导网络服务的多址接入框架，将变长数据包接入建模为α-公平性和能效优化问题；引入自吞吐量和辅助吞吐量新指标量化空间语义冗余；基于无模型多智能体深度强化学习（MADRL）设计PRISM协议，使设备仅凭本地观测即可自主管理频谱接入。

Result: PRISM成功利用语义冗余超越语义无关方案，达到集中式最优基准90%的性能，在不同用户-语义关联矩阵下将目标优化指标提升最高达2倍；验证了分布式语义多址接入的可行性，可与语义域复用的正交多址方案互补。

Conclusion: PRISM为未来分布式移动网络应用提供了可行方案，证明了利用语义空间冗余优化多址接入的有效性，为语义域信号复用技术与传统正交多址方案提供了补充路径。

Abstract: The transition toward semantic-aware communication offers a paradigm shift for next-generation mobile networks, promising to decouple information significance from raw data transmission. Despite advances in semantic extraction, the integration of semantic intelligence into the Medium Access Control (MAC) layer remains underexplored, particularly in exploiting spatial correlations among users. To address this, we introduce a novel multiple access scheme designed for uplink-dominant network services. This framework optimizes the trade-off between spectrum utilization and sustainability by formulating variable-packet-length access as distinct $α$-fairness and energy efficiency problems. A key innovation of our approach is the quantification of spatial redundancies through novel metrics of self-throughput and assisted-throughput, which account for the semantic correlation of data across user equipment. We analyze these formulations to identify optimal bounds before proposing PRISM (Protocol for Redundancy Identification in Semantic Multiple-access). Grounded in Model-free Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning (MADRL), PRISM enables devices to autonomously govern spectrum access using only local observations. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PRISM successfully leverages redundancies to outperform semantic-oblivious schemes, achieving up to \({90\%}\) of the centralized optimal benchmark and improving both objectives by up to \({2\times}\) across diverse user-semantic association matrices. These results validate PRISM as a viable candidate for future distributed mobile network applications, complemented by orthogonal Multiple Access Schemes where signals are multiplexed in the semantic domain.

</details>


### [15] [From Prior-Guided Heuristics to Deployable Agents: Accelerating Demonstration-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Deadline-Constrained Network Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03590)
*Vincenzo Norman Vitale,Mohammad Solki,Antonia Maria Tulino,Andreas F. Molisch,Jaime Llorca*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向部署的网络控制框架，通过引入有效拥塞（EC）指标族和模型引导退火强化学习（MGA-RL）协议，解决了动态异构网络中延迟敏感信息的严格端到端峰值延迟保证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NextG交互式应用需要在动态异构网络上及时交付延迟敏感信息，但提供严格的端到端峰值延迟保证仍然是一个开放挑战。两个主要障碍限制了基于学习的网络控制的采用：传统的基于容量的路由度量虽然对一般流量管理很有效，但并非为捕获流量紧迫性而设计；深度强化学习（DRL）控制器从零开始训练存在样本效率低、训练时间长和早期探索波动性等问题。

Method: 首先，提出了有效拥塞（EC）指标族，这是一种截止时间感知的度量，通过数据包紧迫性量化接口拥塞并主动过滤不可行流量，配合统一路径分组（UPG）分布启发式方法促进鲁棒的负载均衡；由此产生的策略被嵌入到多智能体深度强化学习有效拥塞（MADRL EC（p*））中，这是一种结合分布式调度器和集中式基于RL的路由器的混合架构。其次，引入了一个统一的训练目标，将现有的策略学习范式（行为克隆、离线强化学习、在线强化学习和离线到在线方案）作为特殊情况进行了推广，结合了实时奖励项、预收集奖励项和策略模仿项。由此推导出模型引导退火强化学习（MGA-RL）协议，在深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）骨干上实例化。

Result: 提出了EC指标族能够量化接口拥塞并主动过滤非可行流量；MADRL EC（p*）混合架构实现了分布式调度与集中式路由的有效结合；MGA-RL协议推广了传统的离线到在线（O2O）方案，提供了一种面向部署、演示驱动的训练方法，有效解决了从零开始训练的样本效率和时间问题。

Conclusion: 该框架通过截止时间感知的度量设计和演示驱动的训练方法，成功解决了动态异构网络中严格端到端延迟保证的挑战，为NextG交互式应用的部署提供了可行的解决方案，同时克服了传统学习方法的局限性。

Abstract: Timely delivery of delay-sensitive information over dynamic, heterogeneous networks is essential for NextG interactive applications, yet providing strict End-to-End (E2E) peak latency guarantees remains an open challenge. Two obstacles limit the adoption of learning-based network control in this setting: traditional volume-based routing metrics, while highly effective for general traffic management, are not designed to capture traffic urgency; and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) controllers trained from scratch suffer from sample inefficiency, long training times, and early-stage exploration volatility. This paper introduces a deployment-focused network control framework that addresses both obstacles. First, we present Effective Congestion (EC), a deadline-aware metric family that quantifies interface congestion by packet urgency and proactively filters non-viable traffic, coupled with a Uniform Path Grouping (UPG) distribution heuristic promoting robust load-balancing; the resulting policies are embedded into Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning Effective Congestion ($p^*$) (MADRL EC ($p^*$)), a hybrid architecture combining a distributed scheduler with a centralized RL-based router. Second, we introduce a unified training objective that generalizes existing policy-learning paradigms---behavioral cloning, offline Reinforcement Learning (RL), online RL, and offline-to-online schemes---as special cases, combining a live-reward term, a pre-collected-reward term, and a policy-imitation term. From this objective, we derive the Model-Guided Annealed Reinforcement Learning (MGA-RL) protocol, instantiated on a Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) backbone: a deployment-oriented, demonstration-driven training approach that generalizes conventional Offline-to-Online (O2O) schemes, in which trajectories from a lightweight [...]

</details>


### [16] [Employing the Structural Power to Achieve Supply-Demand Balanced Payment Channel Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03600)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Hongwei Shi,Weicheng Wang,Anlin Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑优化的支付通道网络余额重新配置方法，通过支付拓扑熵指标指导通道结构重组，有效缓解供需失衡问题，显著降低余额短缺和交易失败概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链支付通道网络(PCNs)面临余额供需失衡问题：通道预存余额(供给)需匹配交易需求(需求)，余额不足会导致交易失败。现有方法如交易路径优化和通道余额分配虽能缓解该问题，但为适应动态需求波动需要高昂的成本。

Method: 1. 揭示PCN余额短缺与网络拓扑的关联性，发现流动性分配的结构组织规律；2. 提出支付拓扑熵(PTE)指标，基于信息论量化节点偏离全局平均连接模式的程度，捕捉余额供给的结构特性；3. 设计MaxPTE拓扑优化算法，通过结构重组重新配置通道余额分配，使静态余额分布与动态交易需求对齐，无需预测需求或额外补充余额。

Result: 在多项指标上显著优于现有基准：余额短缺降低27.25%，平均最大流提升12.23%，交易失败概率降低25.96%。方法在不同余额-需求分布下均保持鲁棒性，无需先验需求预测即可改善供需平衡。

Conclusion: 从静态拓扑角度通过通道重新配置可有效缓解PCN供需失衡问题，PTE指标能够准确刻画余额供给的结构特性，MaxPTE算法为PCN优化提供了低成本、高鲁棒性的解决方案，避免了动态适应波动需求的昂贵开销。

Abstract: Blockchain technology faces scalability challenges because transactions must be validated and recorded across the network. Payment channel networks (PCNs) improve efficiency by moving transactions off-chain and recording only critical interactions on the mainnet. However, PCNs require pre-deposited channel balances (supply) to match transaction demands (demand), and insufficient balances cause supply shortages. Existing approaches, including transaction path optimization and channel balance allocation, address this problem but incur high costs due to dynamic adaptation to fluctuating demands. We reveal a correlation between balance deficits in PCNs and network topology by uncovering the structural organization of liquidity allocation. This enables supply-demand conflicts to be mitigated from a static topological perspective through channel reconfiguration, without additional balance replenishment. We further introduce payment topological entropy (PTE), an information-theoretic metric that quantifies each node's deviation from the global average connection pattern and captures structural properties of balance supply. Based on PTE, we design MaxPTE, a topology optimization algorithm that reorganizes balance allocation across channels through structural reconfiguration, aligning static balance distribution with dynamic transaction demand. Extensive experiments show that MaxPTE reduces balance deficits by 27.25%, increases average maximum flow by 12.23%, and decreases transaction failure probability by 25.96%, outperforming existing benchmarks. The method also remains robust across diverse balance-demand distributions, improving supply-demand balance without prior demand prediction.

</details>


### [17] [Is Collision-Free Backoff Worth It in Wi-Fi?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03817)
*Mohammad Yousefi,Francesc Wilhelmi,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统级仿真评估了三种无冲突退避算法在WiFi中的性能，发现在全缓冲流量下仅带来轻微吞吐量提升（≤6.3%），而在非全缓冲流量下无冲突访问甚至可能在下行重度流量下降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管无冲突退避算法有潜力提高WiFi效率和可预测性，但在现实非全缓冲流量（包含上下行传输）场景下，这些方法是否足够改善WiFi性能尚不明确，需要深入调查。

Method: 采用系统级仿真方法，比较三种无冲突算法（CSMA/ECA、CSMA/E2CA和确定性退避DetBO）与标准BEB及单阶段BEB的性能。评估场景包括：全缓冲流量、非全缓冲ON/OFF流量（含上下行传输）、以及与传统BEB站点的共存场景。

Result: 全缓冲流量下，无冲突操作仅提供轻微吞吐量增益（最高6.3%）；非全缓冲流量下，延迟主要由竞争窗口决定而非冲突避免，无冲突访问在下行重度流量下甚至降低性能。站点至少93%的时间回退到随机退避，因此在AP之外很少形成无冲突调度。

Conclusion: 无冲突退避算法在实际非全缓冲流量场景下的性能改善有限，协议设计需要重新考虑其在真实网络环境中的适用性和局限性。

Abstract: The Distributed Coordination Function (DCF)---the underlying channel access protocol in Wi-Fi, based on Carrier Sense Multiple Access with Collision Avoidance (CSMA/CA) and Binary Exponential Backoff (BEB)---is simple and effective, but its performance can degrade with increasing contention due to collisions. Collision-free backoff algorithms replace randomization with deterministic channel access, potentially improving efficiency and predictability. But are collision-free methods, with all their implications in terms of protocol design, sufficient to improve Wi-Fi performance under realistic non-full-buffer traffic with both uplink and downlink transmissions? We investigate this question by comparing three collision-free algorithms, CSMA/ECA (ECA), CSMA/E2CA (E2CA), and deterministic backoff (DetBO), with standard and single-stage BEB. Using system-level simulations, we evaluate full-buffer traffic, non-full-buffer ON/OFF traffic with both uplink and downlink transmissions, and coexistence with legacy BEB stations. Under full-buffer traffic, collision-free operation provides only modest throughput gains, up to 6.3\%. With non-full-buffer traffic, delay is governed primarily by the contention window (CW) rather than by collision avoidance, and collision-free access can even degrade performance under downlink-heavy traffic. Stations fall back to a random backoff at least 93\% of the time, so a collision-free schedule rarely forms outside the AP.

</details>
