Skywing supports asynchronous operation, publish-subscribe communication, managed message handling, and the composition of independent algorithms into complex decentralized workflows. We demonstrate Skywing using representative algorithms from consensus, optimization, and numerical linear algebra. Experiments on the native Skywing runtime include Push Sum and Max Consensus, a composed monitoring and control workflow, resilient Push Sum under delayed communication, and resilient asynchronous Jacobi under malevolent data corruption. These demonstrations show that Skywing supports diverse decentralized algorithms while separating mathematical logic from communication and execution infrastructure. Skywing serves as both a deployment framework for decentralized applications and a research platform for developing resilient mathematical algorithms.

</details>


### [90] [BASP: Communication-Efficient Batch-Aware Sequence Parallelism for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03151)
*Bigyan Ghimire,Jon C. Calhoun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出批感知序列并行方法(BASP)，通过利用批结构划分GPU组来减少通信开销，在LLM长序列训练中实现1.17-1.31倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列并行方法与批次无关，对所有批次大小采用统一的序列划分，导致通信效率低下。长序列LLM训练面临巨大内存和通信需求挑战，需要更高效的序列并行技术。

Method: 提出批感知序列并行(BASP)，根据微批次大小将GPU划分为不相交的序列并行组，减少全对全通信组规模，实现通信局部化，提高训练效率。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100集群上的实验表明，与标准序列并行基线相比，BASP在Llama和Qwen模型上实现端到端训练时间提升1.17-1.31倍，同时保持相同的模型精度和内存使用。

Conclusion: BASP通过利用批结构优化序列并行通信，显著提升长序列LLM训练效率，为大规模语言模型训练提供有效的并行化解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning for large language models (LLMs) is becoming increasingly important, but training over long sequences remains challenging due to massive memory and communication requirements. Sequence parallelism has emerged as an essential technique for addressing bottlenecks in long sequence LLM training. However, we observe that existing sequence parallelism methods are batch-agnostic and apply uniform sequence partitioning across all batch sizes, resulting in inefficient communication. In this paper, we introduce Batch- Aware Sequence Parallelism (BASP), a sequence parallelism approach that leverages batch structure to reduce communication overhead. BASP exploits batch structure by partitioning GPUs into disjoint sequence-parallel groups according to the micro- batch size. This design reduces the all-to-all communication group size, thereby localizing communication and improving training efficiency. Experimental results on an NVIDIA A100 cluster show that BASP improves end-to-end training time by up to 1.17 - 1.31x in Llama and Qwen models compared to standard sequence parallel baselines, while preserving identical model accuracy and memory usage.

</details>


### [91] [Carbon-aware Resource Management for Latency-Sensitive Cloud Computing Environments: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03270)
*Tharindu B. Hewage,Shashikant Ilager,Maria Rodriguez Read,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对延迟敏感云计算环境中的碳感知资源管理进行了全面的文献综述与分类，分析了现有优化方法，识别研究空白并提出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着延迟敏感云工作负载的激增，云服务提供商持续扩展基础设施，导致碳足迹增加，这与净零排放目标相冲突。由于延迟优化的性能导向型部署模式具有刚性，降低其碳足迹面临挑战，因此需要利用应用特定机会的高效技术。

Method: 提出详细的文献分类体系（taxonomy），系统梳理延迟敏感云计算环境中碳感知资源管理的最新研究，基于该分类体系分析现有工作的优化方面。

Result: 对现有工作进行了系统性分析，揭示了当前优化方法的特点与局限，识别了研究领域的关键空白，并指出了未来研究的方向。

Conclusion: 延迟敏感云计算环境的碳感知资源管理需要更多针对应用特性的优化技术，未来研究应填补当前识别的研究空白以实现性能与碳排放的平衡。

Abstract: Proliferation of cloud-based latency-sensitive workloads requires infrastructures tuned to their workload-specific latency constraints. Today, they shape the cloud from a generalized computing platform to diverse workload-specific cloud environments. As the demand for latency-sensitive workloads increases, cloud service providers continue to scale their infrastructure, adversely increasing the carbon footprint and challenging climate-crisis-driven net-zero emission goals. Due to performance-oriented rigid deployment patterns of latency-optimizations, reducing its carbon footprint is challenging. Therefore, efficient techniques that exploit application specific opportunities are needed in that. To this end, we present a detailed taxonomy of recent literature on carbon-aware resource management in latency-sensitive cloud computing environments. Using the taxonomy, we analyze existing works discussing their optimization aspects, identify the gaps, and highlight future research directions.

</details>


### [92] [FlowTT: Exploiting Computation Flow Reuse in Irregular Tensor-Train Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03459)
*Jongmin Seok,Chae Eun Rhee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlowTT是一个流感知的GPU执行框架，通过挖掘前缀共享的计算流来优化TT embedding查找，在Meta基准测试上相比现有方法显著降低了延迟和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TT分解虽然能有效压缩推荐模型中的大型embedding表，但基于TT的embedding查找效率仍然低下。主要原因在于：(1)跨输入索引的部分共享计算流未被充分复用；(2)TT核心顺序收缩过程中间结果需要重复地在片外存储中物化，导致冗余操作和内存流量。

Method: FlowTT将TT gather重构为一组前缀共享的不规则计算流，采用三项关键技术：(1)流对齐的基于前缀的索引分组；(2)融合的TT embedding执行路径，在片上保留中间结果；(3)持久线程调度，结合分块工作窃取和L2检查点机制，在负载倾斜情况下保持复用。该方法协同设计了任务形成、数据缓冲和调度策略。

Result: 在Meta合成推荐基准测试（Meta-240、Meta-480和Meta-788）上，FlowTT相比现有方法始终实现最低延迟。在批大小32,768时，相比EcoRec，推理延迟降低高达42.2%，训练延迟降低高达49.2%，同时实现了最低的推理峰值内存使用。

Conclusion: 暴露前缀共享的计算流是实现高效基于TT的embedding执行的关键。通过协同设计任务形成、数据缓冲和调度策略，FlowTT有效减少了冗余的TT核心操作、全局内存流量和负载不平衡问题。

Abstract: Tensor-Train (TT) decomposition effectively compresses large embedding tables in recommendation models, but TT-based embedding lookup remains inefficient because partially shared computation flows across input indices are not fully reused and intermediate results are repeatedly materialized off-chip between sequential TT-core contractions. We present FlowTT, a flow-aware GPU execution framework that reformulates TT gather as a set of prefix-shared irregular computation flows. FlowTT combines flow-aligned prefix-based index grouping, a fused TT-embedding execution path with on-chip intermediate retention, and persistent-thread scheduling with chunk-based work stealing and L2 checkpointing to preserve reuse under skewed workloads. By co-designing task formation, data buffering, and scheduling with the structure of TT gather, FlowTT reduces redundant TT-core operations, global-memory traffic, and load imbalance. On Meta's synthetic recommendation benchmarks (Meta-240, Meta-480, and Meta-788), FlowTT consistently achieves the lowest latency compared to existing methods. At batch size 32,768, it reduces latency by up to 42.2% in inference and 49.2% in training relative to EcoRec, while also achieving the lowest inference peak memory usage. These results show that exposing prefix-shared computation is key to efficient TT-based embedding execution.

</details>


### [93] [A Technique for Load Shifting Low-latency Applications in Multi-Region Renewables Harvesting via SMT Core Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03297)
*Tharindu B. Hewage,Shashikant Ilager,Maria A. Rodriguez,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软硬件协同设计方案，通过同步多线程(SMT)和深度空闲技术实现逻辑核心静态化，使低延迟应用能参与可再生能源负载迁移，在减少跨区域迁移的同时保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨地理区域的负载迁移策略虽能减少云基础设施碳足迹，但由于广域网(WAN)的高延迟变化，通常无法支持低延迟应用，导致此类应用无法参与可再生能源追踪。

Method: 硬件层面：在两个服务器池中通过深度空闲物理核心配合SMT技术，在能源供应动态变化下保持逻辑核心静态集合，减少工作负载迁移概率。软件层面：在区域内为低延迟应用优先分配静态核心，同时允许尽力而为应用跨WAN迁移，利用SMT核心因硬件多线程导致的较低性能折损特性。

Result: 在真实实验测试平台上使用Azure VM到达痕迹评估，低延迟VM卸载减少80%，P90终端用户延迟变异系数降低43.81%，SMT核心导致的最大延迟性能损失为11.97%。

Conclusion: 该技术成功将低延迟应用纳入负载迁移框架，在显著减少跨区域迁移和改善延迟稳定性的同时，仅带来可接受程度的性能折损，为绿色云计算提供了新思路。

Abstract: Load shifting across geographic regions to chase intermittent renewable energy availability is commonly used in reducing cloud infrastructure carbon footprint. However, it often omits low-latency applications due to high latency variances of wide area networks (WAN) that interconnect regions. This paper addresses accommodating low-latency applications into load shifting by minimizing their shifting across the WAN. We propose a technique using a hardware-software co-design approach. At the hardware level, we conduct server load matching over renewables supply peaks and valleys by deep idling physical cores in two otherwise identical server pools, with one enabling simultaneous multi-threading (SMT) in CPUs. In return, we achieve a static set of logical cores amidst energy supply dynamics, reducing the probability of workload shifting. At the software level, we efficiently chase the static set of cores for low-latency applications within regions while prioritizing best-effort applications to accommodate shifting requirements across WANs. Our approach exploits the lower performance compromise of SMT cores due to their hardware multi-threading. We implement the proposed technique with OpenStack and CPU idle states and evaluate its performance on a real experimental testbed with Azure VM arrival traces. Results show an 80% reduction in offloading low-latency VMs and a 43.81% reduction in coefficient of variation of p90 end-user latency while having a worst-case latency compromise of 11.97% due to SMT cores.

</details>


### [94] [Lantern: Finding Committable Transactions via Back-Propagation on DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03315)
*Denglong Li,Gerui Wang,Tian Guan,Mingchao Wan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Lantern，一种无需先验知识的高性能确定性并发控制协议，通过局部依赖图识别可提交事务，在区块链平台ChainMaker上实现比Aria高4.2倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并发控制协议存在两个主要问题：要么引入非确定性导致主从节点间存在串行执行-重放依赖，要么依赖不切实际的事务读写集先验知识。这限制了高性能事务处理系统的发展。

Method: 提出Lantern确定性并发控制协议，包含三个核心组件：（1）基于局部依赖图识别零出度事务顶点，采用覆盖允许策略按升序安全提交；（2）反向传播机制从汇点到源点迭代传播依赖状态以识别更多可提交事务；（3）冲突无关批次选择（CFBS）处理读-改-写密集场景。

Result: 将Lantern集成到开源区块链平台ChainMaker中，在YCSB和SmallBank基准测试上验证：相比Aria实现高达4.2倍的吞吐量加速，使ChainMaker执行层吞吐量提升至少2.2倍。

Conclusion: Lantern成功解决了现有确定性并发控制协议的局限性，在无需先验知识的前提下实现了高性能事务处理，显著提升了区块链执行层性能。

Abstract: Existing concurrency control protocols either introduce nondeterminism, resulting in a serial execution-replay dependency between primary and replica nodes, or rely on impractical prior knowledge of transaction read-write sets. In this paper, we present Lantern, a deterministic concurrency control protocol tailored for high-performance transaction processing systems operating without prior knowledge. The key insight of Lantern is that all zero-out-degree transaction vertices in the local dependency graph can be safely committed in ascending order using an overwrite-permissive strategy. We further introduce a novel Back-Propagation mechanism that iteratively propagates dependency states from sink to source vertices to identify additional committable transactions. We also propose Conflict-Free Batch Selection (CFBS) for read-modify-write intensive scenarios. We integrate Lantern into the open-source blockchain platform ChainMaker. Extensive evaluations on YCSB and SmallBank benchmarks demonstrate that Lantern achieves up to a 4.2x throughput speedup over Aria and improves the throughput of ChainMaker's execution layer by at least 2.2x.

</details>


### [95] [Iapetus: Content-Aware Hierarchical Scheduling for Collaborative ViT Inference in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03318)
*Yan Chen,Yunxiang Zhang,Guanjun Jiang,Haiquan Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对卫星边缘计算中视觉Transformer的协同推理问题，提出了内容感知的分层调度器，通过智能选择令牌压缩和层级卸载策略，在保证质量目标的前提下显著降低了延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星边缘计算中运行计算密集型视觉Transformer需要分布式资源协作，但模型分层会导致中间激活数据传输开销巨大；现有令牌压缩方法的质量影响依赖于输入内容、模型深度和先前剪枝决策，而层级卸载需要适应时变接触和电池条件。

Method: 提出内容感知的分层调度器，首先筛选星座范围内的选项以保留有限候选集，然后利用质量预测和联合规划将每个候选细化为完整的令牌压缩和层级卸载轨迹；统一目标函数平衡每任务延迟、能耗、质量损失与累积工作负载和电池压力。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin硬件在环测试平台上实现，在5任务/秒的负载下，任务完成率达91.6%（比最强基线MARATD3高26.1个百分点），平均延迟降低53.0%，电池消耗降低70.8%，同时满足质量目标。

Conclusion: 该系统有效解决了卫星边缘计算中视觉Transformer协同推理的传输开销、质量损失与资源约束的权衡问题，显著提升了任务完成率并降低了延迟和能耗。

Abstract: Collaborative inference pools distributed resources to run compute-intensive Vision Transformers (ViTs) in satellite edge computing. Model partitioning enables such collaboration by assigning consecutive layer groups to different nodes, but the large volume of intermediate activation data incurs substantial transfer overhead that can erase its benefit. Token compression reduces downstream computation and activation transfer, but its quality impact depends on input content, model depth, and earlier pruning decisions, while layer offloading must adapt to time-varying contact and battery conditions. We present \sys, a content-aware hierarchical scheduler that screens constellation-wide options to retain a bounded candidate set, then refines each candidate into a complete token compression and layer offloading trajectory using quality prediction and joint planning. A unified objective balances per-task latency, energy, and quality loss against accumulated workload and battery pressures. We implement \sys on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin hardware-in-the-loop testbed and use its validated execution model for constellation-scale trace replay across multiple ViT workloads and constellation settings. At \(5\)~tasks/s, \sys accomplishes 91.6\% of released tasks, 26.1 percentage points above MARATD3, the strongest baseline, while reducing mean latency and battery draw by 53.0\% and 70.8\%, respectively, and meeting quality targets.

</details>


### [96] [Latency-Aware Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows on Heterogeneous GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03335)
*Jinghao Wang,Yifeng Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Yao Lu,Yihui Zhang,Xiaoyang Sun,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出预测引导的多智能体工作流运行时系统，通过预测设备延迟、内存和加载成本来优化异构GPU池上的执行，显著降低完成延迟和GPU资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发多智能体工作流在异构GPU池上运行时面临未来依赖关系和服务状态需求，需要根据时变负载、模型驻留和资源可用性动态选择物理调度单元、模型生命周期操作和资源放置。

Method: 设计预测引导的运行时系统：预测器估计设备特定激活延迟、峰值内存和模型加载成本并通过工作流依赖传播；构建器生成保持语义的融合和模型生命周期替代方案；调度器基于实时池状态联合优化选择、放置和执行顺序。

Result: 在异构GPU池的三种工作流场景下，端到端完成时间和突发到达时的P95完成延迟分别降低高达36.8%和25.9%，每完成会话节省高达24.63 GPU-s资源。

Conclusion: 预测引导的运行时系统通过工作流预测构建和优化物理执行图，在异构GPU环境下有效提升多智能体工作流的调度性能和资源效率，优于现有最先进工作流调度器。

Abstract: Concurrent multi-agent workflows expose future dependencies and serving-state requirements while running on heterogeneous GPU pools with time-varying load, model residency, and resource availability. The logical workflow defines the required computation, whereas its physical scheduling units, model-lifecycle actions, resource ordering, and placement must be selected according to the observed pool state. We present a prediction-guided runtime that uses workflow forecasts to construct and optimize a physical execution graph. Predictor estimates device-specific activation latency, peak memory, and model-loading cost, then propagates these predictions through workflow dependencies to forecast activation readiness and future model demand. Constructor builds semantics-preserving fusion and model-lifecycle alternatives, while Scheduler jointly optimizes their selection, placement, and execution order based on the live pool state. Across a workload spanning three workflow scenarios on a heterogeneous GPU pool, our system reduces end-to-end makespan and overall p95 completion latency under burst arrivals by up to 36.8% and 25.9%, respectively, over state-of-the-art workflow schedulers. It also saves up to 24.63 GPU-s per completed session.

</details>


### [97] [JuPyLive: Seamless Migration of Jupyter Notebook Resources from Laptop to HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03562)
*Sima Attar-Khorasani,Matthias Lieber,Siavash Ghiasvand*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: JuPyLive实现Jupyter笔记本在本地工作站和HPC集群间的一键无缝迁移，无需用户修改代码或学习新语法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除Jupyter笔记本在本地资源与HPC环境间迁移的复杂性，降低HPC集群使用门槛，让跨学科研究者能够轻松扩展资源密集型工作流。

Method: 利用ElasticNotebook管理内存状态迁移，自动化HPC集群资源分配，编排源与目标间的双向远程通信通道，集成HPC状态监控器提供实时资源概览，通过可视化界面实现一键迁移。

Result: 实现了完全自动化的迁移机制，无需用户修改代码、配置或学习新语法，用户可直接在熟悉的Jupyter环境中通过可视化元素发起和监控迁移过程。

Conclusion: JuPyLive弥合了本地工作空间与远程资源的差距，以最小的用户干预实现资源密集型工作流的无缝扩展，进一步推动HPC集群在跨学科研究者中的普及应用。

Abstract: This work introduces JuPyLive, a migration mechanism that enables seamless transition of Jupyter notebooks between local resources of user's workstation and remote resources of high-performance computing~(HPC) environments, while preserving the user experience. JuPyLive eliminates the underlying complexities of migration process, enabling users to freely choose among available local and remote resources, directly within the familiar Jupyter notebook environment via a single click. JuPyLive leverages ElasticNotebook to manage in-memory state migration, it automates resource allocation on HPC cluster and orchestrates required remote communication channels between the source and destination to enable a bidirectional migration. Furthermore, HPC status monitor of JuPyLive provides a live overview of available remote resources, allowing users to make informed decisions on choosing the relevant resources before initiating a migration process. The proposed fully automatic mechanism requires no code changes or configurations by the end user, nor does it demand users to learn a new syntax, instead the migration process can be intuitively initiated and monitored using visual elements from within the Jupyter notebook. By bridging the gap between local workspace and remote resources, JuPyLive offers a seamless experience for scaling local resource-intensive workflows with minimal user intervention, thus further democratizing the usage of HPC clusters among the interdisciplinary researchers.

</details>


### [98] [Every Kernel Is a Join: Automatic Multi-GPU Parallelism for AI Computations in Einsummable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03905)
*Zhimin Ding,Chen-Kuan Liao,Chima Adiole,Brianna Barrow,Fangzhou Du,Yu Hsiao,Ge Huang,Yicheng Jin,Ismail Syed,Chris Jermaine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Einsummable是一个自动化分布式AI计算系统，无需用户编写设备分配或通信操作，在LLaMA transformer blocks上达到8.97ms运行时间，优于手动调优的PyTorch(13.80ms)和vLLM(15.90ms)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多GPU服务器上的AI计算分布式执行是AI系统领域的核心问题。现有方法需要程序员手动编写设备分配、分片注解和通信操作，这既繁琐又难以优化。研究旨在开发一个完全自动化的系统，能够自动发现和优化分布式执行计划。

Method: Einsummable将每个操作建模为关系连接后接张量关系聚合。通过"join-agg specs"暴露操作的可能分解方式，优化器选择全局分解方案以最小化通信成本代理。系统合成拓扑感知的exchange程序来实现分解操作，所有通信和聚合都专门化并在编译时派生，不调用预定义的集合通信原语。

Result: 在8-GPU A100服务器上的LLaMA transformer blocks测试中，Einsummable达到几何平均运行时间8.97ms，显著优于手动调优的PyTorch(13.80ms)和vLLM(15.90ms)，证明了全自动化系统能够超越手工设计实现。

Conclusion: 通过搜索分解而非命名策略菜单，Einsummable能够发现基于网格的自动并行器无法找到的执行计划。完全自动化的分布式AI计算不仅可以实现，而且在性能上能够超越定制化手动实现。

Abstract: Distributing an AI computation across the GPUs of a multi-GPU server is one of the central problems in systems-for-AI. We present Einsummable, a prototype system that accepts a PyTorch-like description of an AI computation and automatically distributes it across a multi-GPU server, with no device assignments, sharding annotations, or communication operations written by the programmer. Einsummable models every operation as a relational join followed by an aggregation over tensor relations, in which the tuples contain sub-tensors. Each operation exposes its possible decompositions through what we call "join-agg specs". An optimizer then selects decompositions across the whole computation to minimize a communication-cost proxy. Because it searches decompositions rather than a menu of named strategies, Einsummable discovers plans that mesh-based auto-parallelizers cannot. Each decomposed operation is implemented by synthesizing an exchange program, which is a topology-aware generalization of Volcano's exchange operator. Einsummable invokes no canned collectives: all communication and aggregation is special-purpose, derived at compile time. Despite being fully automatic, Einsummable can outperform custom-designed implementations. For example, on LLaMA transformer blocks on an eight-GPU A100 server, Einsummable achieves a geometric-mean runtime of 8.97 ms, versus 13.80 ms for hand-tuned PyTorch and 15.90 ms for vLLM.

</details>


### [99] [Barnacle: Adaptive Multi-Leader Scheduling for DAG-Based Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03978)
*Zeno De Angeli,Alexandru Ianov Vitanov,Philipp Jovanovic,Lefteris Kokoris-Kogias,Alberto Sonnino,Pasindu Tennage,Igor Zablotchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Barnacle通过运行时自适应调整DAG共识协议中的领导者数量，解决了多领导者带来的队头阻塞问题，在健康网络中比单领导者降低6-13%延迟，在退化网络中匹配单领导者性能并比静态高领导者数量降低35-56%延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAG共识中多领导者设计本意是降低排队延迟，但实际部署仅使用单领导者，原因是队头阻塞问题：慢速领导者会阻塞管道至少一个超时周期，且当其槽位需要回退到间接决策规则时会阻塞多个波次，此风险随领导者数量增加而加剧。

Method: 提出Barnacle插件，周期性测量已提交DAG上直接规则决定的槽位比例，采用加性增、乘性减策略动态调整领导者数量。测量过程无需额外消息和密码学操作，具有确定性，且可泛化应用于多种DAG协议。

Result: 在拜占庭(3f+1, 5f+1)、崩溃(2c+1)和混合(5f+3c+1)故障模型下验证了安全性和活性。健康网络中延迟比单领导者降低6-13%；退化网络中匹配单领导者性能，同时比静态高领导者数量降低35-56%延迟。

Conclusion: Barnacle成功实现运行时自适应领导者数量调整，在各网络状态下均匹配最优静态配置的性能，已与Sui区块链团队展开集成合作。

Abstract: In DAG-based consensus, all validators propose blocks concurrently, and designated leader blocks drive transaction commit. Having multiple leader slots per round cuts queuing latency, yet production deployments run a single leader because of head-of-line blocking: a slow leader stalls the pipeline for at least one leader timeout, and for several waves when its slot must wait for the fallback indirect decision rule. This risk grows with the leader count. We introduce Barnacle, an add-on that adapts the leader count at run time. Every interval, it measures on the agreed committed DAG the fraction of slots decided as commit by the direct rule, and drives the leader count with additive increase, multiplicative decrease. The measurement requires no extra messages and no cryptography, and is deterministic. Barnacle is generic over DAG protocols; we instantiate it on four protocols spanning the Byzantine (3f + 1, 5f + 1), crash-only (2c + 1), and mixed (5f + 3c + 1) fault models, with proven safety and liveness. Results show Barnacle matches the best static leader count in every regime: in a healthy network its latency is 6-13% lower than a single leader's, and under degradation it matches a single leader while remaining 35-56% below a static high count. We are currently collaborating with the Sui team to integrate Barnacle into the Sui blockchain.

</details>


### [100] [Para-Pipe: Exploiting Hierarchical Operator Parallelism of ML Computational Graphs on SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04168)
*Yujie Zhang,Huiying Lan,Ehsan Aghapour,Zhiyuan Ning,Peng Zan,Weidong Shao,Anuj Pathania,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Para-Pipe框架，在流水线架构中融合算子并行性，有效平衡边缘SoC上深度学习推理的吞吐量和延迟，同时显著提升能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘深度学习应用日益复杂，传统流水线技术虽然提升吞吐量，但无法满足现代神经网络复杂依赖和大量算子并行性带来的低延迟需求。单纯优先流水线或并行执行往往导致另一性能指标下降，需要在两者间做出妥协。

Method: 提出Para-Pipe分层映射框架，将流水线架构内的阶段内和阶段间算子并行性进行集成。通过选择性微调流水线阶段内部和跨阶段的并行度，在吞吐量和延迟之间进行权衡优化，同时显著减少处理器间通信开销。

Result: 在Amlogic SoC（ARM big.LITTLE CPU和GPU）和黑芝麻技术SoC（深度学习加速器和两个DSP）上的评估表明：Para-Pipe生成多个帕累托最优配置，实现吞吐量与延迟平衡；吞吐量优化配置相比纯流水线策略平均能效提升11.0%，相比非流水线并行执行提升23.3%。

Conclusion: Para-Pipe通过智能融合流水线和算子并行性，成功解决了异构SoC上深度学习推理中吞吐量与延迟的权衡问题，在保证性能的同时显著提升了能效，为边缘AI部署提供了有效的优化解决方案。

Abstract: As edge-based deep learning applications become more complex, optimizing performance on heterogeneous System-on-Chips (SoCs) presents unique challenges. Traditional pipelining techniques distributing the computation across different on-chip processing units, while effective for throughput, do not address the latency demands posed by modern neural networks with complex interdependencies and extensive operator parallelism. There is a potential in leveraging operator parallelism to enable concurrent execution across multiple processing units, thereby reducing inference latency. However, prioritizing pipelining or parallel execution often necessitates a compromise, where optimizing one performance metric adversely impacts the other. This paper introduces Para-Pipe, a hierarchical mapping framework that integrates intra- and inter-stage operator parallelism within a pipelined architecture. Para-Pipe navigates the trade-off between throughput and latency by selectively fine-tuning parallelism levels within and across pipeline stages. This strategy can significantly reduce inter-processor communication overhead, significantly improving energy efficiency. Our evaluation demonstrates that Para-Pipe generates multiple Pareto-optimal configurations, achieving a balance between throughput and latency on an Amlogic SoC equipped with ARM big.LITTLE CPUs and GPU, as well as the Black Sesame Technology SoC featuring a deep learning accelerator and two DSPs. More importantly, throughput-optimized configurations under Para-Pipe on Amlogic SoC show an average energy efficiency improvement of 11.0% over purely pipelined strategies and 23.3% relative to non-pipelined parallel execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型在缺乏线索时依赖训练语料库的一元分布作为贝叶斯先验，该先验编码在解嵌入矩阵的单个方向（"无知方向"）中；通过投影可分解预测状态为先验和似然两部分，且该方向具有因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在上下文信息不足时的预测行为，探究模型如何处理不确定性以及是否遵循贝叶斯推理框架。

Method: 识别解嵌入矩阵中编码一元分布的单个方向（无知方向），通过投影将最终预测状态分解为正交的两个向量，分别对应贝叶斯更新中的先验项和似然项，并通过调节先验加载因子λ验证其因果作用。

Result: 在四个模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia）中均发现无知方向存在；先验加载因子λ随上下文信息量增加而递减；分解结果与温度贝叶斯更新精确对应；λ可在不同模型间有意义地比较；调节λ可因果性地引导预测趋向或偏离先验。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测机制具有几何-概率解释：当上下文信息不足时，模型通过解嵌入几何中的特定方向回退到训练语料库的一元先验，该机制可形式化为贝叶斯更新且具有因果活性。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [102] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方程重排方法，将参数化算子学习重构为单一规范算子学习，实现零样本跨参数预测和可监控推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纯数据驱动的参数化模型需要在宽参数范围内大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数，且在训练分布之外可能发生静默失败，缺乏泛化能力和失败预警机制。

Method: 提出方程重排方法，从控制方程解析推导参数诱导的算子变化并将其吸收到有效源项中，将参数化算子学习重构为学习单一规范算子，支持跨参数区域的零样本预测。

Result: 在多参数、非线性和奇异PDE设置中，该方法支持外推，能在共享规范表示中整合稀疏异构数据集，并使用收敛损失作为重排迭代失败的内部预警信号。在核聚变托卡马克高保真模拟中，该框架通过规范域映射统一了四种设备几何形状的电子温度数据。

Conclusion: 方程重排为可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了一条新途径，结合了方程引导的迁移学习、数据效率和可监控推理能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [103] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了协同学习从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的发展，系统梳理了图数据分布场景、统计异质性及算法框架，并指出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习在可扩展性和隐私方面存在根本局限。现有协同学习研究主要聚焦于规则网格结构的欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），无法捕捉现实应用中图结构数据的关系模式。图学习依赖消息传递机制传播节点信息，与协同环境中代理间的信息交换需求天然契合，但该领域的机遇与挑战仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 首先回顾欧几里得数据协同学习的基础原理，从学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度组织现有研究。然后将讨论扩展至图结构数据，引入图分布场景的分类法，刻画相关统计异质性，并制定标准化的问题表述与算法框架。最后系统性地识别开放挑战与有前景的研究方向。

Result: 建立了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协同学习统一研究框架；提出了图分布场景的系统分类法；明确了图数据协同学习中的统计异质性问题；制定了标准化的问题表述与算法框架；系统识别了该领域的关键挑战与研究方向。

Conclusion: 图结构数据的协同学习是一个具有重要研究价值的新兴领域。通过整合现有研究并提出系统化的分类法与框架，本综述为该领域的未来发展奠定了基础，并为解决实际应用中的关系模式学习问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [104] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RoPE-based Transformers中存在由训练长度位置尺度划分的全局位置带（GPBand），据此提出头级混合架构（HwH），以NoPE FA实现全局检索、LA实现局部位置建模，在FA:LA比例低于1:3的情况下显著提升零样本长上下文外推能力并保持强语言建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合架构（FA+LA）的分配方式依赖启发式方法，缺乏基于证据的分配依据；行为探测方法无法提供完整的头部功能分类体系。

Method: 提出两个干预指标：RoPE频率重要性分数（RFIS）测量各频率对注意力分布的影响，RoPE位置依赖度（RPD）隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖；在Qwen3和Llama3.1模型上进行验证，构建完整的检索头与位置头分类体系。

Result: 发现由中低频带分隔的检索头和位置头分类体系，边界随训练长度位置尺度变化（GPBand）；基于此设计的HwH架构在FA:LA比例低于1:3时，保持强语言建模和常识推理能力，同时改善检索性能并显著增强零样本长上下文外推能力，优于Transformer、LA及层级混合基线。

Conclusion: 位置建模应仅在局部进行，全局访问通过位置无关检索实现；两种功能应以头部粒度分配并采用层级特定分配策略；基于原则的混合架构设计是未来基础模型的有前途方向。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [105] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络(GAT)代理模型，直接在TCAD网格上操作，预测漂移-扩散系统的基本未知量，实现了对大规模FinFET器件的快速仿真与多目标设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散输运仿真是新兴FinFET器件设计的主力工具，但计算成本高昂，尤其是3D结构中运行时间随网格复杂度急剧增加，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理模型将固定设计参数映射到少量标量器件指标，丢弃了底层物理机制，在不同器件几何结构和系列间缺乏可迁移性。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络(GAT)代理模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上操作，在每个网格节点预测静电势以及电子和空穴准费米能级——漂移-扩散系统的基本未知量。训练结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数中。作为图结构在网格上操作，代理模型继承了尺寸泛化能力：在少量鳍片网格上训练的模型可直接应用于更大的阵列。深度集成的每节点不确定性驱动主动学习循环，在几秒内筛选大型候选池，仅将信息量最大的设计转发进行完整仿真。

Result: 在多鳍三栅FinFET上与Sentaurus Device对标，代理模型重现三个漂移-扩散场，每个场的RMSE低于1V，单设计吞吐量比完整仿真器高出几个数量级。优势随器件尺寸增长：在对直接仿真来说计算量过大而无法处理的大型多鳍阵列上，推理仍能在单器件1秒内完成。实现了跨越对直接TCAD扫描不可行的器件尺度的帕累托前沿探索。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了传统TCAD仿真的计算瓶颈，通过物理信息图神经网络实现了快速、准确的器件仿真，具备良好的尺寸泛化能力，为大规模多目标器件设计优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [106] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailRL方法，通过优化奖励分布的上尾概率而非平均奖励，使强化学习策略能更好利用稀有高奖励样本，在多个任务上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，但两个策略可能具有相同平均奖励却有非常不同的高奖励rollout概率。在训练和推理中增加采样时，保留高奖励结果的概率质量至关重要，但平均奖励掩盖了这一重要区别。

Method: 提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，将连续奖励转换为一系列二元成功事件：对于每个奖励阈值，计算策略超过该阈值的概率。最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率，其梯度给予稀有高奖励rollout更多权重，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。仅需对advantage函数进行简单修改，与现有强化学习流程兼容。

Result: 在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI定位和代码优化任务上，TailRL能利用稀有高奖励训练样本避免次优解，并产生在推理时能从额外样本中获益更多的模型。

Conclusion: TailRL通过直接优化奖励分布的覆盖度，有效解决了传统平均奖励优化忽视高奖励样本概率的问题，提升了策略在采样增加时的表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [107] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个深度强化学习框架，通过二阶不确定性量化方法，实现了配电网运行风险和异常事件的联合识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网的可靠运行需要在广泛的不确定性下及时识别运行风险和异常事件。在实际操作中，操作员需要识别随机但符合分布条件的内在风险，以及对应异常负载模式、极端天气或网络物理攻击等分布外行为的异常。

Method: 结合分布式和贝叶斯深度强化学习方法，构建二阶不确定性量化方案，将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性（aleatoric）和认知不确定性（epistemic）两个组成部分，分别用于刻画内在风险和分布外异常。

Result: 认知不确定性估计用于驱动训练期间的探索过程和部署期间的分布外检测及后备控制；偶然不确定性估计用于刻画内在运行风险。仿真结果验证了DRL智能体的性能和不确定性量化的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的显式不确定性感知的深度强化学习框架能够有效实现配电网风险与异常的联合识别，为最优配电网运行提供了可靠的决策支持。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty. In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [108] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成过程多样性这一新概念，利用归一化压缩距离衡量语言模型群体的多样性，发现其能有效预测模型间的相关失败，超越语义相似性和能力指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型群体的多样性评估主要依赖语义相似性，仅能捕捉输出含义的差异，忽视了模型失败之间强相关性的问题；需要更基础的方法来评估模型多样性，以确保多模型系统的安全性和鲁棒性。

Method: 基于算法信息理论，采用归一化压缩距离衡量原始模型输出间的距离，并通过置换控制进行残差化处理，从而推断生成过程多样性；在38个语言模型和10个基准测试家族上进行了实验验证。

Result: 该方法识别出语义相似性遗漏的群体结构；在十个基准测试家族中，生成过程多样性与模型对之间的机会校正相关失败呈负相关（跨基准部分秩关联为-0.216，95%区间[-0.309,-0.122]），且在所有基准上估计均为负值。

Conclusion: 生成过程多样性提供了一种新颖且实用的方法，用于调查安全相关场景下多模型系统的多样性，其能有效预测模型间的相关失败，超越了语义相似性和模型能力的解释范围。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [109] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeanGRPO，通过重构数据并行布局和两种无重计算训练调度，解决了扩散强化学习中 rollout 和更新阶段重复计算的问题，在保持优化目标的同时实现最高1.83倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散强化学习方法（如DanceGRPO和FlowGRPO）在on-policy训练中存在冗余计算问题：rollout后需要重新计算选定的timesteps并跟踪梯度。虽然直觉上可以复用rollout的前向计算图，但直接在rollout时保留梯度会带来巨大的内存开销。

Method: 提出LeanGRPO框架，包含两项创新：1）重构数据并行布局；2）设计两种无重计算训练调度：LeanGRPO-Retain在rollout时启用梯度跟踪并直接复用计算图和保存的激活值用于反向传播；LeanGRPO-Reweight在rollout时启用梯度，使用临时优势值立即反向传播单个步骤并延迟梯度同步，轨迹完成后用真实优势值校正梯度。

Result: 在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO框架下，使用FLUX.1-dev和Wan模型进行实验，LeanGRPO实现最高1.83倍的端到端加速，同时完全保留原始优化目标。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO成功消除了扩散强化学习中的冗余计算问题，通过创新的训练调度策略在保证训练效果的前提下显著提升了计算效率，适用于不同模型规模和输入大小的场景。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [110] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一种基于图网络的颗粒动力学模拟器，通过在接触边上存储相互作用历史，显著提升了长时间模拟的准确性和效率，相比现有方法位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积形态误差降低58-89%，同时实现8.9-12.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒动力学的运动特征高度依赖于颗粒间的接触历史，但在接触形成、断裂和重组过程中难以保持这些历史信息。现有模拟器主要将时序信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，无法有效处理动态变化的接触网络，导致长时间模拟精度下降。

Method: 提出TRACE图网络模拟器，将相互作用历史直接存储在接触边上。核心技术包括：(1)每条边维护持久记忆，通过基于注意力的消息传递和门控循环单元更新；(2)边缘身份字典在接触图变化时保持记忆；(3)物理结构化解码器预测法向和切向接触力，执行库仑摩擦限制，施加大小相等方向相反的内部力；(4)采用单步预训练和自回归展开微调的训练策略。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准测试中，TRACE产生稳定、物理一致的长时间预测，准确再现最终沉积几何形态和坍塌过程中释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，长时间展开位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时使用更少参数并保持接近零的颗粒穿透。相比MPM参考求解器，在2D和3D分别实现12.2倍和8.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过将相互作用历史存储在接触边上而非节点上，TRACE有效解决了颗粒接触动态变化导致的历史信息丢失问题，实现了高精度、高效率的颗粒动力学长时间模拟，为学习型物理模拟器的设计提供了新的架构思路。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [111] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLIWBO通过从有限库中选择输入变换来改进GP-UCB的采样效率，在保持收敛保证的同时解决了核函数几何不匹配问题，在多个基准测试中表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB虽然具有收敛速率保证，但要求固定核函数。当原始坐标与核函数的几何特性不匹配时（如对数缩放的超参数或局部峰值），需要输入变换来提高采样效率，但现有GP-UCB证明无法处理可变核函数。

Method: 提出有限库输入变换贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），从有限的光滑输入映射库中根据历史相关规则选择变换。该方法适应输入几何特性以加速学习，在温和假设下保持高概率收敛保证，并给出明确的√(Nε)库大小代价。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，FLIWBO-UCB在几何不匹配情况下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能够逃逸甚至oracle变换期望改进都无法解决的陷阱，恢复手动对数缩放的大部分增益，并在20维MAS设计研究中展示了在昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO成功解决了输入变换与收敛保证之间的理论冲突，为GP-BO提供了既保持理论保证又提高实际性能的解决方案，特别适用于几何不匹配的黑箱优化问题。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [112] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序列变分自编码器(VAE)的物联网网络异常检测框架,通过对抗学习和对比损失实现零样本域适应,在跨域场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网网络异常检测面临设备多样性、缺乏标签数据和跨环境域变化等独特挑战,传统方法难以有效泛化到不同部署场景。

Method: 采用序列VAE架构,引入对抗学习和对比损失联合优化域不变潜在表示和语义结构化嵌入空间;设计编码器和解码器适配层对齐跨域特征分布;提出基于目的地的分割策略建模真实通信结构。

Result: 在跨越工业、企业、通用、智能家居和军事自动化等六个数据集的44个迁移场景上评估,在多个跨域设置中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力,在对比域适应基线上表现竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无需标签数据或原始特征迁移的情况下实现有效的零样本域适应,适用于现实、异构和隐私受限的物联网环境。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic. Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [113] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子导航图（ENC）的向量数据表示为图结构，利用图神经网络（GNN）对ENC更新进行关键性风险分类，实验证明图表示方法能有效提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子导航图（ENC）是用于海上导航的地理空间向量数据集，其维护工作具有挑战性。对ENC中对象的变化进行分类，判断其对航行安全的重要性，对于保障海上安全至关重要。然而，目前尚不清楚哪种消息传递机制、架构配置和图表示方法最适合此类分类任务。

Method: 将空间对象作为节点，其空间和语义关系作为边，将新旧ENC数据集编码为一对图结构，将任务构建为图对分类问题。在此基础上，研究多种GNN架构和模型配置，用于判断图对是否构成对航行安全的关键风险。

Result: 在由海事专家审核的ENC变更数据上训练和评估了多种GNN架构，结果表明基于图的表示方法能够提升ENC更新分类的准确性，为自动化或改进ENC维护工作流提供了可扩展的方法。

Conclusion: 图神经网络在电子导航图变更分类任务中具有优越性能，能够有效辅助海事专家识别对航行安全有重要影响的变更，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [114] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为具有转移不确定性的博弈和随机博弈（CSG）提出了一个PAC学习框架，能够计算社会福利最优的近似纳什均衡或证明不存在精确纳什均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有转移不确定性的博弈和随机博弈（CSG）中，同时解决纳什均衡存在性问题和学习问题是一个重要挑战。现有的方法难以在转移核不确定的情况下提供理论保证的纳什均衡解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个基于PAC学习的框架：1）维护基于数据的转移核L1置信集；2）求解鲁棒CSG计算社会福利最优的ε-纳什均衡；3）使用鲁棒MDP探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖；4）引入纳什边际刻画实现对均衡存在性的原则性推理。

Result: 算法在满足最小可达性条件下，经过多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为O~(R²max H⁴|S|²|A|/(p_reach ε²))。框架要么返回社会福利值ε接近最优的ε-近似纳什均衡，要么提供不存在精确纳什均衡的可靠证明。实验结果显示接近最优性能且样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 成功建立了第一个处理CSG中转移不确定性和纳什均衡存在性问题的PAC学习框架，为多智能体强化学习中的博弈论问题提供了理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


### [115] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TGOPD方法，通过在应用教师模型的密集监督前验证其在每个prompt上的可靠性，解决了传统On-policy蒸馏中教师模型可能提供错误指导的问题，显著提升了训练效果和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的On-policy蒸馏（OPD）对所有prompt统一应用教师模型的token级监督，但未验证教师模型在每个prompt上是否可靠。由于反向KL的模式搜索特性，自信但错误的教师模型会产生强烈的误导性更新。现有的分布代理指标（如熵或师生似然一致性）只能衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果正确性。

Method: 提出Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation（TGOPD）方法，核心原则是在接受密集监督前在prompt级别验证教师可靠性。TGOPD通过少量验证器评分的教师探针估计可靠性，将每个prompt路由：当可靠性检查通过时使用密集OPD，否则使用基于验证器的GRPO。该方法还利用闲置教师容量进行可靠性估计，提高异步OPD中的教师端计算资源利用率。

Result: 在4B和35B规模的学生模型上，涵盖数学、代码和指令遵循任务，TGOPD在所有6个单领域设置上均优于传统OPD，在多领域训练中两个规模上都实现了更高的7基准平均值。在测量的4B单领域运行中，教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%。

Conclusion: TGOPD通过prompt级别的教师可靠性验证机制，有效解决了传统OPD中不可靠教师监督的问题，在多个领域和规模上都取得了性能提升，同时显著提高了教师模型的计算资源利用效率，证明了该方法在蒸馏训练中的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [116] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了因果基础模型（CFMs），这是一种将基础模型范式应用于因果推断的新方法，能够通过上下文学习在全新数据集上估计因果效应，无需模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要为每个新问题设计专门的流程：提出因果机制、选择兼容的估计器并训练。这种方法效率低下且需要大量专业知识。与此同时，机器学习领域已转向基础模型范式：大规模预训练网络可直接应用于新任务。作者旨在将这一范式引入因果推断领域，以简化因果推断流程。

Method: 提出因果基础模型（CFMs），这是预训练的神经网络，利用上下文学习（in-context learning）在完全新的数据集上估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需进行模型参数更新。论文提供了因果推断和机器学习的必要背景知识，并附带了示例代码和Jupyter笔记本。

Result: 成功构建了一个实用的CFMs入门指南，整合了因果推断和机器学习的理论基础，提供了具体的代码实现示例，为研究人员和实践者提供了可操作的技术资源。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型代表了因果推断领域的重要范式转变，通过预训练和上下文学习消除了传统因果推断中针对每个问题设计专门流程的需求，具有重要的实用价值和发展前景。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [117] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ObserverBench基准框架，用于评估内部估计器（观察者）是否足以指导其所引导的干预、控制或安全任务，发现估计精度与动作选择质量并不总是一致，需要同时评估两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机制可解释性方法被用于指导激活引导、电路移除和安全监控等干预措施，但平均准确的内部估计仍可能选择次优动作，因此需要评估观察者是否真正适合其所指导的任务。

Method: 提出ObserverBench基准框架，每个任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、保留案例和损失，分别报告估计精度和所选动作导致的损失；在电路干预和安全分流任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 在GPT-2-small和Qwen2.5-7B的电路干预任务中，成对观察者预测未见效应更准确但不总能选择更好动作，基于动作损失训练的观察者选择损失更低；在安全分流任务中，完美分离违规的分数在违规成本不同时可能错误分配固定干预预算；跨多个模型发现AUROC排名与部署损失不一致，最佳信息源因模型而异；稀疏SAE读数在报告的Qwen面板上落后于层匹配的稠密控制。

Conclusion: ObserverBench提供了固定任务契约、可运行基线和基于表格的提交，用于通过可解释性方法所启用的动作来评估这些方法，证明需要同时评估估计精度和动作质量。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [118] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM通过局部预训练和组合级迁移学习，显著降低了神经算子在域外几何/工况下的适配成本，在Darcy流动和翼型流动任务上相比全域处理模型误差降低36-56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为物理仿真的快速可微代理模型，在训练域外的几何、尺寸或工况条件下精度会显著下降。虽然有监督适配可以恢复精度，但即使对于小规模目标集也需要昂贵的高保真仿真，这促使研究者探索如何协同设计预训练和迁移学习以降低部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM采用两阶段策略：首先在小子域上预训练神经算子预测局部场；然后在新的部署场景下冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合局部预测，实现组合级的迁移学习。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流动问题中，使用16个目标仿真进行适配后，相比同等容量的全域处理模型，LatentDDM在更大域上的误差降低36-56%；在非定常不可压缩翼型流动问题中，该模型在快频率俯仰流动的20步场预测上，无论零样本还是少样本校准后均有性能提升。

Conclusion: 协同设计的局部预训练与组合级迁移学习为物理基础模型提供了有前景的设计原则，能够有效降低神经算子在域外条件下的部署成本同时保持高精度。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [119] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LeanStream框架，通过流式推测与精细化策略优化设备端大语言模型推理，实现GPU计算与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，在降低内存使用4.8-7.5倍的同时提升吞吐量1.6-2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端大语言模型推理在隐私保护和响应速度方面具有吸引力，但面临模型权重远超可用DRAM的挑战。现有系统利用激活稀疏性和权重卸载技术，但存在系统权衡困境：准确的稀疏执行决策需要最新上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要早期预测。这导致现有设计要么串行执行，要么产生冗余权重获取、额外计算和大量缓存开销。

Method: 提出LeanStream流式推测与精细化框架，利用部分GPU结果逐步精细化计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现GPU执行与存储I/O之间的细粒度重叠。该框架在移动端和嵌入式平台上实现了系统原型。

Result: 相比现有设备端LLM推理系统，LeanStream在达到现有最佳吞吐量的前提下，将内存使用降低4.8倍至7.5倍，同时将token生成吞吐量进一步提升1.6倍至2.1倍。

Conclusion: LeanStream成功解决了设备端LLM推理中计算-I/O重叠与决策准确性之间的矛盾，通过流式推测与精细化策略显著降低了内存需求并提升了推理性能，为资源受限设备上的高效LLM推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [120] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵值连续思维链模型中潜在矩阵秩与推理性能的关系，发现模型对秩不敏感，秩截断不影响准确性，揭示了秩盲现象源于展平投影读出机制，并与位置无关性混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值连续思维链模型将推理压缩到潜在标记中，通过d×d矩阵瓶颈路由每个潜在标记。如果矩阵潜在表示通过叠加承载并行推理路径，那么秩应该能追踪这些路径。本文旨在验证秩是否能作为单样本结构可观察量来反映推理能力，以及低秩截断是否会影响需要多组件解决方案的任务准确性。

Method: 在四个训练方案上测试矩阵CODI模型（三个在ProsQA上，一个在GSM8K-Aug上），进行秩k投影消融实验；训练四种不同读出机制（双线性重参数化、双线性加GELU、SVD增强读出、Z Z^T二次读出）；使用线性探针评估Z的预测能力；设置GPT-2 SFT作为阴性对照组。

Result: 秩k投影消融曲线在所有实验中保持平坦，准确率波动在0.6个百分点内；三次重复实验准确率81.0±2.0%，而最终有效秩跨越{4,12,13}，损失函数不奖励任何特定秩；四种非线性读出机制的秩k曲线仍保持平坦；Z上线性探针性能低于原始预训练隐藏状态（AUC 0.673 vs 0.846）；阴性对照组同样产生平坦的秩k曲线。

Conclusion: 矩阵潜在表示的秩不能有效追踪并行推理路径；秩盲现象源于展平后投影的读出机制，而非矩阵瓶颈本身；秩k消融实验将秩盲性与位置无关性混淆，无法区分这两种现象；研究揭示了当前矩阵值思维链模型在结构化推理表示方面的局限性。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [121] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度光流网络与知识蒸馏的静止轨道大气运动矢量（AMV）反演新方法，在消除对NWP背景场依赖的同时实现了高效的全圆盘风场反演，在水汽波段显著优于业务化产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV方法存在核心缺陷：（1）基于窗口互相关的特征追踪计算成本高、覆盖率低；（2）高度估计依赖红外亮温与NWP背景场的循环依赖，导致高度不准确；（3）立体测风虽能通过视差几何独立反演高度，但依赖多卫星重叠且计算昂贵。这些限制阻碍了高质量风场的业务化应用。

Method: 提出两阶段深度学习框架：第一阶段用深度光流网络替代传统窗口匹配进行立体匹配，通过自监督几何残差损失与监督式探空数据重构的联合微调优化模型；第二阶段采用知识蒸馏技术，将需要多卫星重叠的立体教师模型压缩为仅需单卫星输入的学生模型，同时让学生模型学习教师模型的卡方值和高度不确定性用于质量控制，最终实现全圆盘全球覆盖的风场反演。

Result: 通过三重配准验证方法对立体风场和学生模型进行多源验证（探空数据、业务化AMV、ERA5再分析和EarthCARE云剖面）。结果表明：立体风场在6.2、6.9和7.3μm三个水汽波段性能显著优于业务化AMV产品；但在11.2μm长波红外波段存在性能退化。学生模型成功复现了教师模型的质量控制参数并实现全圆盘覆盖。

Conclusion: 深度学习方法有效解决了传统AMV技术的循环依赖、计算效率和覆盖范围问题，为数值天气预报提供了更高质量的卫星风场数据。水汽波段的显著改进表明该方法在特定波段具有业务化应用潜力，但长波红外波段的性能退化仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [122] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究深度学习中的缩放定律是否适用于保险精算定价领域。实验发现不同模型在数据增加时性能都提升，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM在数据缩放上表现最佳，而纯监督的Transformer参数缩放效果弱，需要额外归纳偏置才能有效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的缩放定律描述了模型容量、训练数据和计算量增加时性能的幂律改善趋势。研究旨在探索这种缩放规律是否存在于数据为表格型、异质且充满噪声的精算定价领域，其中经典GLM模型仍是强基准。

Method: 使用真实汽车保险组合数据集，训练不同模型家族，逐步增加训练数据比例并使用多个随机种子。采用样本外泊松偏差作为评估指标（一种基于似然的泊松计数预测损失函数，值越低表示拟合越好）。比较不同架构在数据缩放和参数缩放方面的表现。

Result: 所有模型家族都随数据增加而改善，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM展现出比纯监督表格Transformer和标准MLP基线强得多的数据缩放能力。Transformer变体在纯参数缩放上表现微弱，除非增加额外归纳偏置（如TabM风格适应或自监督学习）。简单增加Transformer规模带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 研究结果为不同数据规模下的模型选择提供了定量指导，表明在精算表格任务上的有效缩放依赖于架构设计和损失函数目标设计，而非简单地增加模型规模。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [123] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种算法（NTK-KIP、MetaQuill和MetaQuill-KIP），成功将神经切核（NTK）回归方法扩展到非线性可元学习领域，实现了在稀疏观测下高质量的神经场重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场在稀疏观测下的快速高质量重建仍具挑战。经典的神经切核（NTK）回归虽可给出闭式拟合，但本质上为线性方法，无法积累可复用的任务先验。作者希望填补这一空白，在保持NTK优势的同时实现非线性和可元学习特性。

Method: 提出三种算法：1）NTK-KIP学习蒸馏支撑集，使有限NTK能够从少量观测数据修补大块缺失区域；2）MetaQuill通过元学习获得共享初始化，使新场景仅需更新小的任务特定权重偏移；3）MetaQuill-KIP融合两者，用KIP风格的非线性热启动初始化任务，然后在元学习初始化周围细化小的偏移量。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测条件下实现了高PSNR重建和语义合理的修复效果，仅需轻量级逐实例适应。相比依赖大规模预训练生成先验和昂贵逐图像调优的扩散模型基线，该方法展现出明显优势。

Conclusion: 研究证明，基于NTK的神经场可以被赋予非线性和可元学习特性，成功缩小了解析核方法与实际少样本重建应用之间的差距，为神经场快速重建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [124] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE+LoRA中的token级路由分离无法防止负迁移，提出SpawnLoRA通过在专家内部动态添加门控子适配器来解决此问题，有效减少了多领域微调中的负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作假设MoE+LoRA中的token级路由可以将不同领域的更新有效分离，从而避免多领域微调中的负迁移问题。然而，这一假设缺乏实证验证，研究者需要探究路由分离是否真正能够防止领域间的干扰。

Method: (1) 使用Python代码、生物医学文本和数学推理三个领域数据测试MoE+LoRA的路由分离假设；(2) 提出Jaccard路由重叠度和适配器梯度余弦相似度两个诊断指标，分别测量专家共享程度和更新兼容性；(3) 设计SpawnLoRA方法，在检测到适配器级竞争时，在MoE专家内部动态添加门控子适配器，同时保持路由器固定；(4) 在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B模型上进行评估。

Result: (1) 即使领域间专家路由近乎分离，添加生物医学数据仍显著增加代码困惑度，证明路由分离单独无法防止负迁移；(2) 诊断发现干扰主要源于正交的领域梯度在同一低秩适配器子空间中竞争；(3) SpawnLoRA在多个混合设置下相比标准和秩自适应LoRA有效减少了负迁移；(4) 专家内部结构分离带来的收益超过单纯路由或秩扩展。

Conclusion: 路由分离不足以防止多领域微调中的负迁移，问题根源在于不同领域的梯度在同一适配器子空间中竞争。通过在专家内部动态添加结构化分离机制（SpawnLoRA），可以有效缓解这一问题，证明了专家内部结构分离的重要性超越了单纯的路由机制或秩扩展策略。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [125] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿大语言模型作为批量优化器在连续和离散场景下的表现，发现其在数值测试函数上表现良好但脆弱，而在语义丰富的离散空间中显著优于传统方法。
